## 3. Mathematical verification of the decisive steps

### 3.1 Forward isometries genuinely become a group

The compact metric completion `X-hat` is obtained from GP1 total boundedness. Each isometric embedding `f:X-hat→X-hat` is onto: if `y` avoids the closed image, let `δ=d(y,f(X-hat))>0`; for `n>m` the distances of iterates obey `d(f^m y,f^n y)=d(y,f^(n−m)y)≥δ`, contradicting compactness. Thus the forward monoid lies in the compact isometry group. Compact recurrence of powers plus bi-invariance supplies inverse elements in its closure. **No inverse symbol or Lie structure was assumed.**

### 3.2 Lie upgrade uses a real input, not merely finite dimensionality

The theorem `p2:thm:frc-automatic-compact-lie-closure` requires a **faithful continuous finite state-affine operation port** and proves a faithful homogeneous `GL(r+1,R)` representation; a compact closed matrix subgroup is Lie. Its own qubit `SO(3)` carrier-lift obstruction immediately confirms that this **does not** identify a faithful complex representation of the same group on the original qubit carrier. The master theorem's Lie statement must therefore be read in the explicitly declared affine-operation or independently Lie-certified sector.

### 3.3 Exact source globalization vs a quotient

For local Lie type `su(2)`, the source simply connected group is compact `SU(2)`; the full `1⊕ρ` paired model shows finite faithful realizability, including the center. The quotient `SO(3)` imposes the additional equality `−1=e` on the common source presentation. Under the **explicit GP2 relation-exactness semantics in this paper**, it is excluded at the GP2 filter; this is distinct from GP3 comparison. It does **not** follow from compactness alone, nor is it claimed for a different generated role requiring the quotient relation.

### 3.4 Constructed endpoint candidates preserve the distinction between candidate data and prior data

`p2:thm:primitive-endpoint-lift-reachability` does not compress the original finite representation. It constructs a **different complete** finite paired model from a subrole of the source group's skeleton and then checks it against the primitive role. In the nonabelian case, the proof first excludes pure semisimple central kernels, then embeds a simple-root `SU(2)` into the semisimple source and hence into the parent. The primitive simple coroot is used to exclude an unwanted `±1` kernel. The proof retains no chosen root, basis, or axis in the prior inventory. This is the actually consumed GP3 comparison mechanism and is not dimensional minimization.

### 3.5 Round metric is unique only up to scale

The adjoint automorphism group of `su(2)` is `SO(3)` acting irreducibly on the real 3-dimensional algebra. Automorphism-invariant positive quadratic forms form one ray, generated by minus the Killing form. Left translation gives the round metric on `SU(2)≅S³`; the radius remains free. The proof does not identify the original candidate comparison metric with the Killing geodesic distance.

## 4. Explicit boundaries to protect in later batches

**D1 — State-affine/Lie operation-port provenance.** Theorem `p2:thm:geometric-existence-exactification` says **compact topological** always and **compact Lie** if a faithful finite state-affine port or other Lie certificate exists. The cumulative chronology discusses a *declared finite state/test affine-operation (or independently Lie-certified) sector* and elsewhere notes that without it the Lie upgrade needs a separate certificate. T1's opening paragraph says *every surviving candidate* has compact Lie parent. **Audit action:** in the next batch verify that T1's `Adm_R` is indeed explicitly restricted to the state-affine/Lie-certified sector, and trace the generation of that port as actually used. Until then, mark **NEEDS SOURCE/DOMAIN VERIFICATION**, **not a certified theorem failure**. Do not construct a non-Lie counterexample outside the actual allowed paired-model category.

**D2 — Endpoint role faithfulness.** The endpoint reachability statement explicitly requires that the `U(1)` or `SU(2)` endpoint realizes *every intrinsic diagram of `Geo_R`*, which it declares automatic only in the **Paper-II primitive domain**. The role definition excludes independently constituted extra directions/brackets/relations/tests. **Audit action:** test the assertion `automatic` against that domain's exact schema, not against a newly invented richer role. No contradiction has yet been established in-domain.

**D3 — G0 formulation compatibility.** The source's labeled G0 (at 1244) is a detailed actualization/geometry-licensing predicate, while the user's intended minimal G0 is **“Nontrivial complete geometry exists.”** Per the supplied protocol this is a **formulation-compatibility and eventual foundation-minimality task**, not automatic invalidation of the scoped source theorems. Whether the strong primitive-role existence assertion follows from that minimal sentence is a *separate* origin question, not silently part of T1.

**D4 — Physical occurrence of the order-sensitive root.** `p2:cor:operational-momentum-role-occurrence` (2675 onward) explicitly **assumes an already G0-occurring primitive order-sensitive transfer root** and then proves co-realization of its generated momentum parent/carrier. `p2:def:momentum-branch-specialization` (8654 onward) names the noncommutative branch. This does not prove that all nontrivial geometries are order-sensitive or that a named real-world transfer token occurs. Its physical-occurrence antecedent must be distinguished from the mathematical parent theorem. No extra `G0-MOM` witness should be invented; the same occurring root already supplies generated descendants.

**D5 — Stronger multiplicity not inherited from T1.** Actual current Paper II-B `p2:thm:momentum-host-H1` (1112 onward) proves six **type-support** sectors and identifies them with the Paper I-A intrinsic `H₁`; `p2:thm:m1-component` (1367 onward) derives `m_p=1` from highest type/word length even for arbitrary positive implementation multiplicities. Its stronger `p2b:thm:momentum-carrier-rigidity` (1436 onward) explicitly lists additional GP3 *whole-candidate* comparison hypotheses. **These distinct results must not be collapsed.** The general Paper II-B proofs have not been fully re-audited in this batch; only their statements and directly cited short proofs were inspected to establish the correct boundary.

**D6 — DAG coverage.** v1_12 source anchors match the current archive, but the historical 143 staged semantic DAG items have **not** been globally re-audited. Local checks here do not promote their review flags or globally certify 30-paper completeness.

## 7. Exact target, consumed premise, and source-side result

**T2.1 — Lie assertion inside the master theorem:** `p2:thm:master-compact-role-allocation`, source **7814–7823**, **7837–7839**, proof **7881–7886**. Its ambient candidate class is

\[

## 8. Mathematical stress test: finite state embedding does **not** imply an affine port

This is a diagnostic of the *missing implication*, **not** an alleged member of the full GP2-admissible candidate class (which additionally carries represented operation typing).

Let `X=S^1` with any translation-invariant circle metric, let `G=S^1` act by angle translation, and take a qubit finite algebra `A=M_2(C)`. For fixed `0<ε<1/√2`, put

\[
j(e^{i\theta})=\tfrac12\bigl(I+\varepsilon\cos\theta\,\sigma_x+
\varepsilon\sin\theta\,\sigma_y+
\varepsilon\cos(2\theta)\,\sigma_z\bigr)\in S(A).
\]

The Bloch-vector norm is at most `ε√2<1`, so these are genuine states. The first two coordinates make `j` continuous and injective, hence an embedding of compact `S^1` into a *finite-dimensional* state space. The connected compact geometric `G` action is effective and isometric. Its represented map through `j` need not be affine: for rotation by `φ=π/4`, an affine extension `A_φ` on `aff_R j(X)` would make its third coordinate `cos(2θ+π/2)=-sin2θ` an affine combination of

\[
1,\quad \cos\theta,\quad \sin\theta,\quad \cos(2\theta),
\]

which is impossible by orthogonality of Fourier characters. (These functions span the coordinate-affine functions on `j(X)`.) Thus an additional *generated operation port* is mathematically load-bearing; it cannot be inferred merely from a faithful embedding, compactness, or finite state-space dimension.

This example deliberately does **not** claim that `G=S^1` is non-Lie (it is Lie), nor that this presentation passes the framework's full GP2 operation-envelope faithfulness. Its purpose is to rule out an invalid one-line proof of affine automaticity. The source itself states the same safeguard at **1602–1604** and **1697–1707**.

## 9. Two source-compatible repair routes and what is actually proved

### R1 — Direct finite-operation certificate (fully proved as an implication)

**Lemma (finite faithful continuous operation representation suffices).** Let `M` be a GP1/GP2-admissible geometric realization with compact exact-operation group `H` from `p2:thm:gp1-bounded-isometry-closure`. If its **generated, role-faithful operation sort** supplies a continuous injective homomorphism

\[
\rho_M:H\longrightarrow GL(V_M),\qquad \dim_{\mathbb R}V_M<\infty,
\]

then `H` is compact Lie. **Proof:** `ρ_M(H)` is compact in the Hausdorff matrix group, hence closed. The closed-subgroup theorem makes `ρ_M(H)` a finite-dimensional real Lie group, and `ρ_M` is a homeomorphism onto its image because it is a continuous injection from compact to Hausdorff. Therefore `H` is Lie. Haar averaging yields an invariant positive form on `V_M` and a finite unitary complexification. Nothing asserts a carrier-linear lift to the original `H_M` unless that is the supplied operation port. ∎

**Relation to the source:** this is a sufficient *weaker alternative* to the affine port already allowed in firewall **1703–1707**, not a new physical principle. If the source's GP1 **finite represented operation envelope** and GP2 **continuous exact host faithfulness** really provide `ρ_M` for every `M∈Adm_R`, the master theorem's apparently missing Lie inference is discharged **without changing the allowed candidate class**. The remaining work is a typed proof of *that* extraction; a finite algebra by itself is not an extraction.

**No theorem-wide closure is asserted yet:** neither `p2:thm:canonical-finite-state-test-evaluation` nor `p2:thm:state-dual-affine-operation-reconstruction` demonstrates universal `ρ_M` for comparison-isometry operations. Conversely, the existence of `ρ_M` cannot be denied merely because the carrier-linear lift is unavailable: its domain may be the finite generated test/operation span rather than the original ontic Hilbert carrier.

### R2 — Compare through Lie images rather than prove every parent is Lie (partial reduction, not a replacement proof)

The already established **Peter–Weyl lemma** yields the following even if a compact connected candidate parent `H` is not initially known to be Lie:

* **Abelian:** a nontrivial character maps `H` *onto* `U(1)`; the role is still connected, nontrivial, abelian.
* **Noncommutative:** choose `a,b∈H` with `[a,b]≠e`. Point separation supplies a finite-dimensional unitary `ρ` with `ρ([a,b])≠I`. The connected image `L=ρ(H)` is a **noncommutative compact Lie group**. Its semisimple Lie algebra contains a compact root `su(2)` subalgebra by `p2:lem:a1-least-local-reduction` (**7406–7440**). `p2:prop:admissible-globalization-operator` (**7612–7689**) gives `E_adm(su(2))=[SU(2)]`, and `p2:lem:relation-exact-endpoint-models` (**7442–7497**) supplies a complete finite endpoint model.

This proves **existence of the finite Lie reduction and a valid abstract endpoint model** without assuming original `H` is Lie. It **does not yet prove** that every such reduction furnishes a GP3 comparison inside the original set of *complete*, role-faithful, GP2-relation-exact intermediate candidates. A quotient `L` may carry an ungenerated central relation and require exactification before it enters the structured preorder. Source **6664–6722** demands that *every comparison step* have valid structured endpoints; source **7691–7792** currently constructs the subgroup directly in Lie `H`, rather than checking this general compact-topological quotient route.

Accordingly, **R2 is not a claimed closure of the universal candidatewise reachability theorem**. It is a precise, already-proved algebraic reduction plus *one* explicit structured-comparison obligation. It is not a separate speculative particle/carrier construction.

### 12.1 Exact necessary operation-port contract (source-check, not an added axiom)

For a literal all-`Adm_R` compact-Lie conclusion, the missing explicit typing would be an existing **generated** operation sort supplying a real or complex finite vector space `V_M` and a **continuous faithful action of the closed parent**:

```
ExactOp(H_M) --rep_M--> Aut_lin(V_M) ⊂ GL(V_M),  dim V_M < ∞
rep_M(hk)=rep_M(h)rep_M(k);  ker(rep_M)={e};
rep_M is continuous for the same GP2-generated action topology.
```

The contract must connect the left side to the **whole closed `H_M`**, not merely to a dense forward monoid, an unrelated finite operator set, a continuous test map on `X_M`, or a compression of parent differential operators. Once connected, the original source's compactness and the closed-subgroup theorem give the compact Lie conclusion **without any choice of metric or physical marked representation**. This implication itself is already mathematically established in Batch 2 §9 (R1); Batch 3 makes explicit which **typed extraction** is required and why `Q` is not a substitute.

The source's standard state-dual theorem, `p2:thm:state-dual-affine-operation-reconstruction` **1059–1131**, reconstructs finite linear test operators **from an already affine** state action; it does **not** turn arbitrary comparison isometries into affine state operators. Likewise, `p2a:cor:finite-state-test-dual-dynamics` **1133–1175** starts with an affine operation family. This is consistent with the firewall and prevents circular reasoning.

## 13. Adversarial Q/compression check (E4 for the *shortcut*, not the master theorem)

Take `U_theta=diag(exp(i theta),exp(-i theta))∈U(2)`, `v=(1,1)/sqrt(2)`, `P=|v><v|`. Ordinary compression to `ran P` gives

`Q(U_theta) = P U_theta P |_{ran P} = cos(theta) I`.

Therefore `Q(U_{pi/2})=0` while `Q(U_{pi/2} U_{pi/2})=Q(U_pi)=-I`, so

`Q(U_{pi/2}) Q(U_{pi/2}) = 0 ≠ -I = Q(U_pi)`.

Compression may not even take invertibles to invertibles. It is **not** a group representation absent an invariant-subspace/equivariance contract. This test is a correct counterexample to the **invalid auxiliary implication** "declared compression ⇒ exact faithful group representation"; it is **not** claimed to be a GP2-admissible candidate for an exact-operation role, since the GP2 restriction/compression typing rule is explicit at **2924–2928**. The test therefore **does not refute** the master theorem and does not reopen Paper II-B `H_1` or `m_p=1`.

## 14. Independent replay: the abelian endpoint does NOT require the universal Lie upgrade

**Existing claim independently verified within the paper's primitive endpoint domain:** Let `M∈Adm_R` be any complete primitive candidate with **nontrivial connected compact topological** parent `H` and `epsilon_R=ab`, without assuming `H` Lie. These hypotheses follow from `p2:thm:geometric-existence-exactification` **6376–6454** and the primitive role `Geo_R` **6289–6340**.

1. By the paper's `p2:lem:peter-weyl-finite-image-reduction` **7374–7404**, compact abelian nontrivial `H` has a nontrivial continuous character `chi:H→U(1)`. The connected compact image is a nontrivial connected closed subgroup of `U(1)`, hence **all `U(1)`**. The image retains the primitive scalar role's connectedness, effectiveness (under the regular action) and commuting order-swap diagram.
2. The paper's full GP3 preorder `p2:def:global-gp3-preorder` **6664–6722**, elementary comparison **(i)** explicitly allows **passage to an effective continuous connected homomorphic image**, provided every admitted endpoint is a complete GP1/GP2-exact role-faithful model. The character image itself is the already correct global group `U(1)`; its local type `u(1)` is returned as `U(1)` by `p2:prop:admissible-globalization-operator` **7656–7660**. There is **no non-admissible intermediate group** and no source-restoring semisimple quotient to cross in this abelian one-step chain.
3. `p2:lem:relation-exact-endpoint-models` **7442–7497** supplies a **new** complete, finite, faithful, exact paired `U(1)` model, with the regular parent action. The construction is independent of the original finite-carrier presentation, which GP3 **does not require** at the target (**6681–6686**). In the declared primitive scalar domain the endpoint realizes all intrinsic `Geo_R` diagrams (verified in Batch 2 §10); no source-only tests or relations are erroneously frozen.
4. Consequently, there exists a complete endpoint `M_*` with `Skel_R(M_*)≅U(1)` and `M_* ≼_R M` for **every** such candidate, regardless of whether the original `H` is Lie. A connected image/subrole strictly below `U(1)` that retains a nontrivial connected abelian role does not exist; neither does a role-preserving factor deletion. Thus the `U(1)` primitive skeleton is least in this enlarged topological-parent source domain.

This is a **verification of the paper's already claimed abelian endpoint**, not a new physical mechanism or a replacement for the structured GP3 preorder. The independent proof checks the complete target endpoint, uses exactly the existing source definitions, and removes one proposed auxiliary dependence on universal `H`-is-Lie. It does **not** prove compact Lie status for arbitrary `H`, the physical occurrence of the abelian role, or any extra carrier uniqueness.

**Important scope guard:** The result abstains whenever the role has richer intrinsic tests, brackets or toral directions that an isolated `U(1)` endpoint fails to realize, exactly as the master theorem already does at **7833–7835**. The `Adm_R` source remains the complete primitive-role candidate class, not the class of all arbitrary compact topological groups with no role geometry.

## 15. Noncommutative branch: remaining issue is a complete exactification/comparison route

From a compact connected **non-Lie** noncommutative `H`, `p2:lem:peter-weyl-finite-image-reduction` supplies `H→L` onto a noncommutative compact connected **Lie** image `L`. The Lie root construction `p2:lem:a1-least-local-reduction` **7406–7440** gives an `su(2)` local subrole, and the GP2 globalization and model lemmas **7612–7689**, **7442–7497** supply the **complete** endpoint `SU(2)`.

The GP3 rule **6687–6705** exactifies the local germ before admitting **each** comparison endpoint. It remains to check whether a candidatewise finite-image step `H→L`, followed by `E_adm(Lie(L))` and a root-subrole step, can always be represented by a sequence of *complete admissible structured endpoints* without illicitly treating a source-form restoration as a homomorphic image of `H`. The current endpoint theorem **7719–7792** instead takes an `SU(2)` subgroup inside `H` after assuming `H` is compact Lie and applies the no-gratuitous kernel argument.

No asserted counterexample is supplied: `SO(3)` quotient pathologies alone are not models of the unmarked **relation-exact** primitive parent and are insufficient to refute its claim. The honest status is **unresolved outside the already source-certified Lie comparison sector**.

# Batch 4 — Noncommutative Peter–Weyl exactification and complete GP3 endpoint

**Audit date:** 8 November 2026. **Framework source:** `Paper2A-GeometricConstitution-v3_205.tex` from `Paper00-PapersMinimal-v2_356`, SHA-256 `8b24ab435935dd39785ef92402f6cf531612b8cf51596b1404f1ae3d28ccfd12`. **Original theorem:** `p2:thm:primitive-endpoint-lift-reachability` at **7691–7792**, consumed by `p2:thm:master-compact-role-allocation` at **7891–7938**. **Claim type:** existence of a complete, role-faithful endpoint candidate below *each* admissible unmarked primitive noncommutative candidate; conclusion concerns the **least operation-role skeleton**, not a canonical full structured lift, physical occurrence, or preferred finite carrier.

## 19. Source-directed correction to the Batch-3 noncommutative uncertainty

Batch 3 (§15) correctly rejected treating source-form restoration as an *ordinary homomorphic image*. But it did not yet use the **full** explicitly defined GP3 elementary comparison grammar. Definition `p2:def:global-gp3-preorder` at **6664–6722** says (i) passage to an effective continuous connected homomorphic image, (ii) construction of a complete exact candidate from a closed connected local subrole, or (iii) removal of an unused direct factor; crucially **6687–6705** require passing *the retained local germ of the proposed endpoint* through `E_adm` **before admitting that endpoint**. On a set-valued branch **6701–6703** allow the existence of an appropriate member to witness a comparison without freezing/choosing that member as physical data.

Therefore the operation authorized by the source is **image of the parent, followed by candidate-internal exactification of the image germ**. It is **not** a newly invented GP3 cover operation and does **not** require a homomorphism from the old parent onto the exactified covering group. This order of operations makes the Batch-3 proposed non-Lie route fully tractable. The source's Lie-parent assertion remains a *separate* problem.

This audit uses the definition as printed. If the comparison preorder were later redefined to require the *final exactified endpoint group itself* to be an actual homomorphic image of the old group, this proof would not follow. That would be a **different, stricter preorder** than **6687–6705**, and is not silently introduced here.

## 20. Lemma B4.1 — Nonempty `E_adm` class for every finite noncommutative Lie image

**Statement.** Let `H` be a nontrivial connected compact topological group with a nontrivial commutator, and let `L` be any finite-dimensional noncommutative connected compact Lie image of `H` produced as in `p2:lem:peter-weyl-finite-image-reduction` **7374–7404**. Write

\[
 \mathfrak l=\operatorname{Lie}(L)=\mathfrak z\oplus\mathfrak s,
 \quad \mathfrak s\ne0,\quad r=\dim\mathfrak z.
\]

Let `S` be the compact simply connected integration of `s`. Then `K_ex(l)` as defined at **7612–7632** is nonempty, including if the source simply connected integration `R^r × S` is noncompact.

**Proof (independent E3 algebraic replay).** Let `Λ=Z^r ⊂ R^r`, choose any full-rank lattice, and let

\[
 \Gamma_0=\Lambda\times\{e\}\subset\mathbb R^r\times Z(S),
 \qquad C_0=(\mathbb R^r\times S)/\Gamma_0\cong T^r\times S.
\]

`C_0` is compact and connected, has Lie algebra `l`, and its kernel satisfies

\[
 \Gamma_0\cap(\{0\}\times Z(S))=\{e\}.
\]

By `p2:lem:relation-exact-endpoint-models` **7442–7497**, every compact connected Lie group, in particular `C_0`, admits a faithful finite-dimensional complete paired FRC model on `C ⊕ V` with the center detected in the conjugation action. Thus `C_0` belongs to the source-defined relation-exact class `K_ex(l)` under its **7612–7632** kernel criterion, and `E_adm(l)` is not FRC-obstructed. The particular lattice is an existential *mathematical witness*; its basis, period and representation are not inserted into `P_{<R}`. `E_adm` may remain a non-singleton unselected family. QED.

**Scope qualifier:** `K_ex` is the source's **defined** admissible-globalization class. This lemma establishes nonemptiness for compact-reductive Lie germs by an actual paired model and the declared pure-semisimple-kernel test; it does not independently rederive every semantic GP2/no-insertion rule for arbitrary future role grammars. The endpoint proof applies only in the original primitive role domain.

## 21. Lemma B4.2 — Uniform `SU(2)` subgroup in **every** exact globalization

**Statement.** For every `C ∈ K_ex(l)` (or any representative of its unmarked class), `C` contains a closed embedded group isomorphic to `SU(2)`.

**Proof.** Every compact connected globalization of the compact-reductive Lie algebra `l` is

\[
 C\cong(\mathbb R^r\times S)/\Gamma,
 \quad\Gamma\subset\mathbb R^r\times Z(S)
\]

with discrete central `Γ`. The defining `K_ex` test gives

\[
 \ker\bigl(S\xrightarrow{s\mapsto[(0,s)]} C\bigr)
 =\Gamma\cap(\{0\}\times S)
 =\Gamma\cap(\{0\}\times Z(S))=\{e\}.
\]

Thus `S` is isomorphic to a compact (hence closed) subgroup of `C`. Since `s≠0`, choose one compact simple factor of `S` and one simple root `α` of that factor. The root homomorphism

\[
 \phi_\alpha:SU(2)\to S
\]

has derivative the compact root `su(2)`; its kernel is central and is at most `{±1}`. In the **simply connected** compact semisimple factor, the cocharacter lattice equals the coroot lattice, and a **simple coroot** is primitive. Its maximal circle therefore has trivial kernel, excluding `-1`. Hence `φ_α` is injective (same root argument as **7759–7776**). Composing `SU(2) ↪ S ↪ C` is a continuous injection of a compact group into a Hausdorff group, hence an embedding onto a closed subgroup. QED.

**Why this is stronger than a choice-dependent existence proof.** Even when `E_adm(l)` is a *family* (e.g. mixed lattice–center compactifications), **every** member contains an `SU(2)` root subgroup and hence leads to the **same least skeleton**. No physical section/member of that family is selected. This applies the source's whole-family/no-selection guard rather than hiding it.

## 22. Theorem B4.3 — Full noncommutative primitive GP3 endpoint bypass (new derivation, existing objects)

**Claim.** Let `M ∈ Adm_R` be any complete candidate within the source's *unmarked Paper-II primitive noncommutative role*, including a candidate whose parent is known only to be a **compact connected topological** group. Assume, exactly as the original theorem requires, that the displayed endpoint realizes every intrinsic `Geo_R` diagram; the original theorem calls this automatic in the Paper-II primitive domain at **7709–7713**. Then the **as-written** `p2:def:global-gp3-preorder` establishes a complete exact paired `M_*` with

\[
 \boxed{M_*\preceq_R M,\qquad \operatorname{Skel}_R(M_*)\cong SU(2).}
\]

The all-candidate Lie-parent upgrade is **not needed** for this skeleton conclusion.

**Proof.**

1. **Candidatewise compact source and noncommutative status.** `p2:thm:geometric-existence-exactification` **6376–6454** together with `p2a:lem:v281-connected-generated-group` **6342–6374** supplies a nontrivial compact connected **topological** parent `H` of `M` and the GP2-derived order-sensitive branch. No Lie hypothesis is used in this step. Source `M` remains a full GP1/GP2 complete structured candidate and retains all its own data.

2. **Exact image-germ comparison.** Choose `h,k∈H` with `c=[h,k]≠e`, solely as a proof witness. By `p2:lem:peter-weyl-finite-image-reduction` **7374–7404**, a finite-dimensional unitary representation `ρ:H→U(N)` has `ρ(c)≠I`, hence its connected compact Lie image `L=ρ(H)` is noncommutative. The continuous surjection `π:H↠L` is an elementary **image witness** of type (i) at **6675–6677**. The image germ has semisimple derived Lie algebra and the same primitive noncommutative role status.

3. **Complete endpoint of the first comparison.** The GP3 rule **6687–6705** does not admit the *raw* `L` without exactification; it applies `E_adm(Lie L)` first. By Lemma B4.1 that class is nonempty. By **7442–7497**, each resulting compact globalization has its own faithful finite represented action, regular effective connected isometric parent comparison geometry and complete internally generated/retained relations and tests; it can be made a paired exact candidate `M_C`. In the primitive domain the required intrinsic diagrams are nontriviality, connected local generation, and the order-sensitive branch, all of which are preserved. The full source candidate's extra self-operations or metric profiles are **not** cross-candidate requirements, per **6664–6686**. Thus the grammar explicitly admits `M_C \preceq_R M` as an image-germ step **followed by candidate-internal exactification**. **No homomorphism `H→C` is asserted.** If `E_adm` returns a family, the source permits existential chain witnesses without freezing a family member.

4. **Complete subrole comparison, uniformly.** Lemma B4.2 supplies an **embedded closed** `SU(2)` root subgroup of **every** exact `C`. Definition **6677–6679** permits construction of a new complete exact paired model from this local subrole, and **7442–7497**, **7656–7660** give its relation-exact globalization `E_adm(su2)=[SU(2)]`. Its regular action, represented exact operations, complete generated relations/tests and GP1 boundedness meet the primitive role. Let it be `M_*`. Then `M_* \preceq_R M_C` by the **subrole** step. Its root and a choice of faithful finite representation are proof witnesses, not frozen marks.

5. **Composition and leastness.** By reflexive transitive closure **6707–6721**, `M_* \preceq_R M_C \preceq_R M`, hence `M_* \preceq_R M`. If `M` is already a relation-exact `SU(2)` skeleton the comparison is an equivalence. If a different skeleton has a compact Lie parent, source **7922–7934** proves no smaller admissible noncommutative `SU(2)` image, subgroup or factor deletion (the `SO(3)` quotient fails GP2 relation exactness). If `M` has non-Lie compact parent, there is no reverse GP3 chain `SU(2) → M`: finite chains starting from `SU(2)` and using connected Lie images, closed connected Lie subgroups, factor deletions and the `E_adm` Lie globalization step remain Lie. Thus a non-Lie skeleton is strictly above the endpoint. The common `SU(2)` skeleton is irreducible and least in the same role-specific GP3 preorder. QED.

**Dependencies explicitly not used:** an affine extension of arbitrary comparison isometries, a finite faithful representation of **all** of `H`, a group homomorphism from `H` onto a source-restored central cover, GP3 dimension minimization, the physical selection of a root or lattice, and Paper II-B carrier multiplicity.

## 23. Adversarial checks (distinguish an invalid shortcut from the allowed comparison)

### E4-A — Why direct source restoration is *not* a homomorphic image

The admissible source `H=SU(2)` has a finite-dimensional Peter–Weyl image through its adjoint representation `Ad:SU(2)↠SO(3)`. `SO(3)` has a nontrivial pure semisimple central kernel relative to its local `su(2)` source, so the raw image does **not** pass the primitive relation-exact endpoint filter. `E_adm(su2)=[SU(2)]` repairs it. There is **no** nontrivial continuous homomorphism `SO(3)→SU(2)`: a nonzero derivative would be an isomorphism `su2→su2`, which would give a covering of simply connected `SU(2)` by `SO(3)`, impossible. Hence one must not claim the exactified group is itself a quotient image. This invalid shortcut is avoided by the **image-germ then exactify** rule *already written* at **6687–6705**. The test refutes a shortcut, not the GP3 result.

### E4-B — Nonunique exactification is not a selector gap

For the compact reductive local type `u(2) ≅ u(1) ⊕ su(2)`, both `T^1×SU(2)` and `U(2)=(R×SU(2))/<(π,-I)>` satisfy the pure-semismple kernel test `Γ∩({0}×Z(SU2))={e}`, have faithful finite paired models, and are non-isomorphic as compact Lie groups (their centers have different numbers of connected components). `E_adm` may therefore retain an unresolved family for this type. **Both** groups contain a closed embedded `SU(2)` and reach the same exact least skeleton. Nothing in the proof freezes a choice between them.

### E4-C — No claim that `Q_M` is a faithful group representation

The source's parent-to-finite realization map `Q_M` **5805–5832, 6487–6515** remains only what it is declared to be. Its possible compression/quantization nature is unchanged. Batch 3's explicit compression counterexample still blocks the shortcut `Q_M ⇒ continuous injective representation of full H`; the new endpoint proof makes no such inference.

### 26.1 Candidate-domain decision (not a speculative stronger theorem)

The displayed `Adm_R` is *literally* the conjunction of `Geo_R`, GP1, candidatewise exact presentation and faithfulness to `P_<R` (**6460–6473**). No named operation-port map is included in that display. The subsequent **6505–6508** statement calls the action a *derived candidatewise conclusion rather than an admissibility hypothesis*; **6520–6524** says the derivation is conditional. Treating 6505–6508 as a **new admissibility stipulation** would change what it claims to derive and contradict the latter qualification unless a prior theorem is located that proves this stipulation from the displayed predicates.

The actual source does **not** identify the parent-to-finite map `Q_M` with an operation homomorphism; cf. the exhaustive `Q` check in Batch 3. The source also does not establish the single total map

\[
\rho_{\mathfrak M}:H_{\mathfrak M}\longrightarrow GL(V_{\mathfrak M}),\qquad
\rho(gh)=\rho(g)\rho(h),\quad
\ker\rho=\{e\},\quad
\rho\text{ continuous},\quad \dim V<\infty,
\]

for **every** model in that displayed class. Therefore the *source-level inference* from the conditional exactification theorem to the universal Lie preamble is **not discharged by the proof as written**. This is a **certified missing inference/typing certificate** for the universal Lie clause. It is **not** an in-domain falsifying countermodel to `Adm_R` or a refutation of the role endpoint.

### 26.2 The precise sufficient bridge, with no new axiom

Either of these existing-theorem-compatible witnesses would discharge the clause for a candidate:

**State/test port:** supply `j: Xhat -> S(A)` continuous injective, every forward isometry `s` as an affine automorphism `A_s` of `aff_R(j(Xhat))`, preserve composition and generated action topology. Then `p2:thm:frc-automatic-compact-lie-closure` yields a faithful continuous `rho_aff:G -> GL(r+1,R)` with `r+1 <= dim_R A_sa <= dim_C(H_ont)^2`. This is precisely what **1593–1664** already proves, not a novel principle.

**Finite-operation port:** prove that the actual entire closed `H_M` is represented by a **continuous injective group homomorphism** into the finite represented exact-operation envelope `A_M^× ⊂ GL(H_ont)` (or some other finite `GL(V)`). Compactness makes this matrix image closed; the closed subgroup theorem then makes `H_M` Lie. The missing work is proving this **complete closed-parent identification** rather than inferring it from the mere existence of a finite envelope, a parent comparison action, GP2 continuity, or `Q_M`. A separately certified Lie source is also allowed by the present theorem.

Neither route requires a carrier-linear representation on the **original Hilbert carrier** when a faithful finite state/test representation already suffices. In particular, the source's qubit `SO(3)` lift obstruction **1666–1695** does not refute this state-span route.

## 27. Independent E4 stress test: finite state embedding plus faithful connected isometries can have a non-Lie closure

This tests **only** the proposed invalid shortcut “finite `j` + GP1 total boundedness + connected faithful isometries -> the isometry closure is Lie”. It is **not asserted to lie in all of literal `Adm_R`**: source-specific GP2 realization of every *operation* in its **generated sort** is precisely the unresolved port issue, and an explicit finite operation representation is absent in this example.

For `n>=1`, let `r_n=2^{-n}`. Define the compact metric space

\[
 X=\{\infty\}\sqcup\coprod_{n=1}^\infty C_n,\qquad C_n\cong S^1,
\]

with

\[
\begin{aligned}
 d(\infty,x)&=r_n && (x\in C_n),\\
 d(x,y)&=r_n|z_x-z_y| && (x,y\in C_n,\ z_x,z_y\in S^1),\\
 d(x,y)&=r_n+r_m && (x\in C_n,\ y\in C_m,\ n\ne m).
\end{aligned}
\]

The triangle inequality holds because the within-circle chord is at most `2r_n`, exactly the cost through `infinity`; all cross-circle paths are no shorter than the direct `r_n+r_m` distance. Because `r_n -> 0`, any sequence of points in circles with index `n -> infinity` converges to `infinity`, and each individual circle is compact; hence `X` is compact, so GP1-TB is satisfied.

Let

\[
 H=\prod_{n=1}^\infty S^1
\]

act by independent rotation of `C_n`, fixing `infinity`. This action is faithful and isometric. The uniform isometry metric is

\[
 D_\infty(g,h)=\sup_{n\ge1}r_n|g_n-h_n|.
\]

Because `r_n -> 0`, the uniform topology on `H` agrees with the product topology. Thus its closed exact isometry image really is the compact group `H`. The group is connected, locally path-connected (product-basic open neighborhoods may be chosen connected), and generated by finite words from any product-basic connected identity neighborhood containing full tail factors. It is **not Lie**: every identity neighborhood contains a nontrivial subgroup supported in a sufficiently high coordinate, contradicting the no-small-subgroups property of finite-dimensional Lie groups. It has the abelian order-swap status and an effective nontrivial comparison action, exactly the abstract `Geo_R` features relevant to this test.

There is even a **faithful continuous embedding into a finite complex state space**. Define a planar compact embedding

\[
 j(\infty)=0,\qquad
 j(C_n)=\{(c_n+\varepsilon_n\cos\theta,\varepsilon_n\sin\theta,0):\theta\in\mathbb R\},
\]

where `c_n=2^{-n}` and `epsilon_n=2^{-2n-4}`. These disjoint small circles converge to zero and lie inside the open unit Bloch ball; identifying Bloch vectors with states of `A=M_2(C)` makes `j:X -> S(A)` a continuous injection. Compactness makes `j` a homeomorphism onto its image, whose inverse is uniformly continuous: the comparison metric satisfies a modulus `d(x,y)<=omega(||j(x)-j(y)||)` with `omega(t)->0`. Therefore **finite carrier + finite state space + continuous faithful state embedding + GP1-TB** still do not force Lie structure.

The missing ingredient is genuinely **affineness of the operation action**. A nonidentity rotation of one circle `C_n` fixes every point of some other circle `C_m`. Any affine map on the two-dimensional affine span `V=aff(j(X))` that fixes three noncollinear points of `j(C_m)` must be the identity on all of `V`, so it cannot rotate `j(C_n)`. Thus even a single nontrivial coordinate rotation has **no affine extension on `V`**. This is an explicit obstruction to replacing the state-affine *hypothesis* in the source's Lie theorem by the other displayed geometric/finite-state hypotheses.

**Scope firewall:** This is a mathematically valid **E4 shortcut countermodel**, **not** a certified fully GP2-admissible paired model. We have not provided an all-operation `H -> A^×` faithful host map or a proof that the role's generated operation sort permits a separate infinite comparison-action port. It therefore does not refute the literal master theorem; it establishes why the missing port is a real proof obligation instead of a harmless omitted lemma citation.

## 28. Independent discharge: the set-sizedness assertion does not need Lie

The last sentence of `p2:def:cumulative-role-allocation` **6591–6593** justifies the candidate preorder's set-sizedness by “finite ontic carrier and a compact Lie parent.” Its **conclusion** can be checked independently using only the actually established compact **metric** parent; there is no need to route this downstream result through the missing all-parent Lie certificate.

Fix the finitely presented role signature from `p2:def:finite-presentation-convention` **855–868** and the finite carrier semantics **870–927**. Every compact metric comparison `Xhat` is separable, so choose a countable dense sequence `(x_i)`. Its isometry type is determined by the matrix `(d(x_i,x_j))_{i,j in N}` of real numbers, a member of the set `R^(N x N)`. Conversely, the completion associated with any admissible such matrix is unique up to isometry. Each isometry of `Xhat` is determined by its values on that countable dense sequence, so it can be coded as a member of `Xhat^N`, also a set. A closed subgroup `H ≤ Isom(Xhat)` and its action can therefore be coded by subsets/functions built from these sets. The finite algebra, carrier and finitely generated role presentation are likewise encoded by matrices and finite-schema relations on sets of uniformly bounded cardinality. The set of all these codes and their isomorphism classes is a **set**, not a proper class.

**Verdict:** the actual **set-sized preorder** conclusion survives without any full-parent Lie theorem. This is an independent verification of an *existing stated conclusion*, not a new physical structure or assumption. Only the **Lie-based justification sentence** is over-restrictive; the conclusion needs no weakening. This uses the fixed role/signature assumption already in the source; it does not assert a set-sized universe across arbitrary external proper-class signatures.

## 29. Positive-dimensional Lie witnesses versus positive-dimensional Lie parent

`p2:thm:gp1-gp2-positive-dimensionality` **8244–8262** is valid as a **conditional Lie-group statement**: if a compact Lie `G` is nontrivial and connected, then `dim(G)=dim(G^0)>=1`. Its “Consequently every GP1/GP2-admissible realization ... has a nonzero Lie algebra” at **8252–8254** requires the universal Lie certificate and is **not** established for any arbitrary compact topological parent by the proof **8257–8261** alone.

But the operationally useful **finite Lie-image witness** is already available without that stronger premise: any nontrivial connected compact topological `H` has, by Peter–Weyl separation, a nontrivial finite-dimensional continuous unitary image `L`; the image is connected compact Lie and hence `dim L >= 1`. In the noncommutative branch, `p2:lem:peter-weyl-finite-image-reduction` **7374–7404** gives an **NC** image separating a commutator. This is a conditional-use/scope correction, not a claim that `H` itself has a finite-dimensional Lie algebra. Batches 3–4 use exactly the finite *image*, not the stronger blanket Lie status.

Thus **one canonical origin issue** has distinct outcomes at downstream uses:

- **Master preamble 7819–7823:** unresolved universal full-parent Lie claim / missing typed proof.
- **`E_adm` proof 7665–7667:** its own definition starts from an already exposed compact-reductive local **Lie type** (**7614–7618**); the blanket parent-Lie sentence is unnecessary for those inputs, but not a universal result about all `Adm_R`.
- **Original endpoint proof 7719–7776:** used the universal Lie shortcut; independently verified **abelian and NC primitive role** alternate GP3 proofs bypass it; no reinstatement of the shortcut.
- **Positive dimensionality 8244–8262:** conditional compact-Lie theorem verified, but the all-candidate parent wording still needs the port; a finite positive-dimensional Lie *image* witness is independently assured.
- **Set-sized preorder 6591–6593:** the conclusion is independently verified by compact-metric coding, so this need **not** remain marked blocked by Lie.

## 33. E3: mathematical distance-profile separation and its physical guard

**Target:** `p2:thm:canonical-metric-profile-separation`, **7318–7372**, using `p2:thm:gp1-bounded-compactness` **6922–6935** and `p2:def:metric-partial-evaluation-closure` **7296–7316**.

For any compact metric completion `(\widehat X,\widehat d)`, define `b_z(x)=\widehat d(z,x)`. The triangle inequality gives

\[
 |b_z(x)-b_z(y)|\le\widehat d(x,y),\qquad
 |b_x(x)-b_x(y)|=\widehat d(x,y).
\]

Therefore `\lVert b_x-b_y\rVert_\infty=\widehat d(x,y)`. If isometries `R,S` differ, take `x` with `Rx\ne Sx` and `z=Rx`. Then `(R^*b_z)(x)=0` while `(S^*b_z)(x)=\widehat d(Rx,Sx)>0`. This proves mathematical state and operation separation **without** GP3 or Lie regularity. It does not assert that every `b_z` is a physical affine effect: physical B-test availability requires the explicitly separate metric-curry G0/grammar occurrence, **7296–7316**.

**Verdict:** `CLOSED WITHIN CLAIMED SCOPE` — mathematical representation on function profiles; physical B tests remain conditional. This verifies the theorem and guards against adding an unlicensed finite-dimensional interpretation.

## 34. E4: full metric profiles need not form a finite-dimensional affine test port

**Counter-shortcut, not a framework countermodel.** Take the compact geodesic circle `X=S^1` with angular coordinate `\theta` and metric `d(\theta,z)=\min_{k\in\mathbb Z}|\theta-z+2\pi k|`. Its canonical distance profiles are the translates of the even `2\pi`-periodic function `f(\theta)=|\theta|` on `[-\pi,\pi]`. Its exact Fourier series is

\[
 f(\theta)=\frac{\pi}{2}-\frac{4}{\pi}
   \sum_{\substack{n\ge1\\n\ \mathrm{odd}}}
      \frac{\cos(n\theta)}{n^2}.
\]

Every odd harmonic has nonzero Fourier coefficient. Taking continuous Fourier projections of the translate orbit `\{f(\cdot-z):z\in S^1\}` isolates `\cos(n\theta)` and `\sin(n\theta)` for **every odd** `n`. If this orbit spanned a finite-dimensional subspace of `C(S^1)`, its (automatically closed) span would contain all those linearly independent harmonics, a contradiction. Hence

\[
 \dim_{\mathbb R}\operatorname{span}\{d(z,\cdot):z\in S^1\}=\infty.
\]

But for a fixed finite-dimensional `C^*`-algebra `\mathcal A` and **one fixed state embedding** `j:S^1\to S(\mathcal A)`, all affine evaluations `x\mapsto j(x)(a)` (`a\in\mathcal A_{\rm sa}`) span at most `\dim_{\mathbb R}\mathcal A_{\rm sa}` dimensions (see **929–1003**, especially 993–1001). **Consequently the entire geodesic-circle profile family cannot be represented as affine test evaluations on that one finite algebra.** The profile theorem provides a faithful action on a generally infinite-dimensional function space, not automatically a finite-dimensional Lie certificate.

A numerical cross-check of the Fourier coefficients by trapezoidal quadrature (400001 uniform samples) returned odd coefficients `a_1=-1.273239544761`, `a_3=-0.141471060552`, `a_5=-0.050929581816`, agreeing with `-4/(\pi n^2)` to `3e-11`; `a_2\approx0`.

**Scope:** This proves the invalidity of the particular **full-profile => finite affine test-space** shortcut. It does **not** refute GP1/GP2-admissibility of the selected circle skeleton, prohibit a separately generated smaller faithful matrix port (which a circle certainly possesses), or deny the source's conditional physical B-test doctrine. Nonlinear test channels or parent comparison presentations need not be affine evaluations of the same fixed finite algebra unless that typing is explicitly claimed.

## 35. E3: commutator theorem and GP3 no-generation corollary

**Targets:** `p2:thm:gp2-commutator-dichotomy` **8053–8109**, `p2:cor:gp3-no-generation-alone` **8111–8131**. Source prerequisites: the role's intrinsic order-swap diagram **6289–6340**, typed host faithfulness **2914–2939**, and — for named distance-profile witnesses only — `p2:thm:canonical-metric-profile-separation` **7318–7372** plus metric-curry **7296–7316**.

If `H_R` is nonabelian, there exist `r,s` with `c=[r,s]\ne e`. A transformation-separating test action `\rho_R:H_R\to\operatorname{Aut}(\mathcal B_R)` is faithful; hence `\rho_R(c)\ne\rho_R(e)` and there is **some** `b\in\mathcal B_R` with `c^*b\ne b`. Conversely, a changed test rules out `c=e`, so `H_R` is nonabelian. Any homomorphism `H_R\to C` to a one-dimensional connected Lie group kills every commutator (because such `C` is abelian); a faithfully represented commutator witness cannot factor through `C`. No parent-Lie condition or assumed test-profile family is needed for this **algebraic** equivalence.

The paper's separate assertions are consistent:

1. **NC branch** is an algebraic statement about the exact generated group, even without a physical B-test occurrence.
2. **Physical B-witness** requires constituted transformation-separating test doctrine. If the doctrine lacks this condition, the theorem does not assert a physical observable.
3. **Distance-profile B-witness** additionally requires metric-curry closure; the mathematical full-profile family does not count as a finite affine port by itself.
4. **GP3** cannot introduce a missing test: under GP2 a required test must already be generated and retained; otherwise the candidate is disqualified for this role, not repaired by minimization.

**Verdict:** Both source claims `CLOSED WITHIN CLAIMED SCOPE`, with the physical occurrence guard retained. No dependency on `BH-IIA-PORT-001` exists for the algebraic proof, and this cannot be used in reverse to discharge the missing affine port.

## 36. E3: independently checked downstream SU(2) metric comparison, with imported scope

**Target:** `p2:cor:primitive-killing-metric-comparison` **7982–8027**, requiring the **same** derived `SU(2)` skeleton and a retained primitive comparison satisfying **(FD)+(LR)**. Its actual imported inputs are `p2:cor:gp1-first-order-norm-derived` (Paper II-F v1.56 **2683–2699**) and `p2:thm:gp1-propagation-derived` (Paper II-F v1.56 **2568–2663**). This is an indispensable **interface** check, not a claim to have audited all of Paper II-F or independently reproven its external intrinsic-metric classification.

Given those imported results, `F` is a finite positive Ad-invariant norm on `\mathfrak{su}(2)`. Since `\operatorname{Ad}SU(2)=SO(3)` is transitive on every `g_K`-unit sphere, positive homogeneity forces the same norm on all directions:

\[
 F(X)=\beta\sqrt{g_K(X,X)},\qquad \beta>0.
\]

Its unit ball is already an ellipsoid. Thus the paper's John metric is `g_F=\beta^2g_K`. The trace form of every **faithful** finite-dimensional unitary proof representation is positive and Ad-invariant, so is `g_\rho=a_\rho g_K`, `a_\rho>0`. Therefore `D_F=\beta d_K`. Paper II-F's stated estimate `D_\infty\le D_F\le C_R D_\infty` near the identity gives the local comparison. On compact `SU(2)`, the ratio `D_F(e,g)/D_\infty(e,g)` is continuous away from `e` and bounded outside the local neighborhood; bi-invariance yields the global constant `C_K` as claimed.

**Verdict:** `CLOSED WITHIN DISPLAYED (FD)+(LR) AND IMPORTED PAPER-II-F THEOREM SCOPE` for the algebraic/metric pushforward. No equality `D_\infty=d_K`, universal GP1-only first-order norm, selected radius, or unproved physical operation rate is inferred. Further foundational scrutiny of the imported Paper II-F intrinsic-metric theorem belongs to a separate targeted verification, not an automatic defect in II-A.

## 39. Independent E3 reconstruction of the canonical evaluation theorem

Let `A = ⊕_{λ=1}^r M_{d_λ}(C)` be a unital finite-dimensional C\*-algebra. Its coordinate-independent normalized **abstract** block trace is `Tr_A(x)=Σ_λ Tr(x_λ)` for this chosen direct-sum presentation. Nondegeneracy of the bilinear trace pairing gives for any complex-linear functional `ω` exactly one block matrix `ρ` such that `ω(a)=Σ Tr(ρ_λ a_λ)`. Positivity of `ω(vv*)` in each block for arbitrary `v` makes each `ρ_λ` positive Hermitian; conversely `ρ_λ≥0` makes `ω(a*a)≥0`. `ω(1)=1` is exactly `Σ Tr(ρ_λ)=1`. The positive trace-one block densities are compact convex by elementary finite-dimensional norm boundedness.

For Hermitian `a`, spectral bounds `−||a||1≤a≤||a||1` give `|ω(a)|≤||a||`. Pick a unit eigenvector for a block eigenvalue with absolute value `||a||`; the rank-one state concentrated in that block attains the bound. This proves injectivity, norm preservation and order separation of `Γ:a↦(ω↦ω(a))`.

**Surjectivity without a hidden continuity hypothesis:** For an *arbitrary algebraically affine* `f:S(A)->R`, define on the positive dual cone `K=A_{sa}^{*+}`

`\tilde f(0)=0`, `\tilde f(φ)=φ(1) f(φ/φ(1))` for `φ≠0`.

For `φ,ψ∈K` the decomposition `(φ+ψ)/(φ(1)+ψ(1))` is their weighted convex combination, so `\tilde f(φ+ψ)=\tilde f(φ)+\tilde f(ψ)`. It is positive homogeneous for nonnegative scalars. Since every Hermitian functional is a difference of two positive functionals, define `L(φ−ψ)=\tilde f(φ)−\tilde f(ψ)`. If `φ−ψ=φ'−ψ'` then `φ+ψ'=φ'+ψ`; cone additivity makes `L` well-defined. Thus `L∈(A_{sa}^*)^*`, which is `A_{sa}` in finite dimension, yielding a **unique** `a` with `f(ω)=ω(a)`. No continuity of `f` needed as an additional premise. Testing positive spectral projections proves `0≤E≤1` iff `0≤ω(E)≤1` for every state. For a unital *-homomorphism `φ:A->B`, state pullback `η↦η∘φ` gives the exact naturality identity, with the correct contravariant arrow direction.

**Verdict:** all five subclaims of **975–1057** hold for the precise stated algebra/state domain. The mathematical existence of the state space is not the physical occurrence of a chosen state or measurement.

## 40. Independent E3 duality/Jordan and dynamics proof

For `F:S(A)->S(B)` affine and any `b=b*∈B`, `f_b(ω)=F(ω)(b)` is a real affine function on `S(A)`; section 39 uniquely represents it by `a_b=a_b*∈A`. Real linearity, positivity and unitality of `b↦a_b` follow from state separation; extend complex-linearly. Its dual identity determines the map completely. For a unital positive complex-linear `T:B->A`, `ω∘T` is a state of `B`; reversing the construction recovers `T`, proving the two assignments are inverse.

If `F` is bijective affine, `F^#` and its inverse are positive unital linear, hence give a bijective affine map between the effect intervals. Their extreme points are exactly projections: spectral values in `(0,1)` admit two-sided perturbations, whereas a convex decomposition of a projection `p` into effects forces both effects to equal `p` on its range and to vanish on its kernel. Since `p+q≤1` for projections is equivalent to `pq=0`, the order isomorphism preserves orthogonality. Applying it to the finite spectral resolution of any `b=b*` gives `F^#(b²)=F^#(b)²`; polarization gives the Jordan product identity, and complexification gives the Jordan *-isomorphism. Multiplicative *-isomorphism or complete positivity **does not** follow.

For an existing monoid family `F_{mn}=F_m∘F_n`, evaluate against each state `ω`: `ω(F_{mn}^#a)=F_m(F_nω)(a)=ω(F_n^#(F_m^#a))`. State separation proves **`F_{mn}^#=F_n^#∘F_m^#`**, source **1145–1177**. This orientation is verified with noncommuting stochastic matrices in the independent check. If `M=R_{≥0}` is additive, commutativity makes the dual law ordinary semigroup composition, **but supplies no negative-time operation**.

**Verdict:** source theorem **1059–1131** and monoid corollary **1133–1182** are mathematically correct and make exactly the qualified category/physical claims printed.

## 41. Independent adversarial E4 checks: sharp non-implications (not countermodels)

**(A) Positive/Jordan dual does not imply complete positivity.** On `M₂(C)`, transpose `T(b)=b^T` is a unital positive map, and its state dual is `F(ρ)=ρ^T`. It preserves `b∘c=(bc+cb)/2` but reverses multiplication: `(bc)^T=c^T b^T`, not generally `b^T c^T`. For `|Φ⁺⟩=(|00⟩+|11⟩)/√2`, the partial transpose `(id₂⊗T)(|Φ⁺⟩⟨Φ⁺|)=(1/2)SWAP` has eigenvalues `(1/2,1/2,1/2,−1/2)`. Thus `T` fails 2-positivity and CP. Source **1089–1096** expressly excludes CP and associative orientation; the example **validates the limitation**, not refutes the theorem.

**(B) Unique algebra density does not imply unique carrier density.** Take `H=C²`, algebra `A=C I₂ ⊂ End(H)`. `S(A)` has exactly one abstract algebra state `ω(λI₂)=λ`, represented by block density `ρ_A=(1)` in `M₁(C)`. But the distinct carrier density matrices `D_0=|0⟩⟨0|` and `D_1=|1⟩⟨1|` both satisfy `Tr_H(D_i λ I₂)=λ` for all algebra tests. Therefore **980–990** never yields uniqueness of a state preparation/density on the larger carrier; **955–957** and **1050–1056** explicitly do not claim it. This also prevents using abstract affine-test duality as a shortcut for a faithful linear lift to `H` or a carrier-sector selection.

**(C) Dual action composition order is genuinely contravariant.** On `A=C²`, the row-stochastic positive unital maps represented by `T₁=[[1,0],[1/2,1/2]]`, `T₂=[[1/3,2/3],[0,1]]` do not commute. With row state `ω`, let `F_i(ω)=ω T_i`. Then `F_1(F_2ω)=ω(T₂T₁)`, different from `ω(T₁T₂)`, confirming the exact order in **1151**, **1171–1177**. The particular matrices are **finite-state controls**, not new physical dynamics.

**Exact control script:** `Proofs/Paper2A-StateTestBatch7-v1_1-verify.py` (included in v1.17 DAG Atlas), with successful symbolic positive-block trace, norm, transpose/Jordan, Bell spectrum, stochastic anti-composition and restricted-state tests. The script is an adversarial *sample*, not a proof of the universal assertions above; the general arguments are given in §§39–40.

## 42. No new gap: explicit operational typing and existing port issue

The mathematical order/Jordan duality is **not an inference** that every comparison-parent operation or GP1 compact isometry acts affinely on the full finite algebra state space. For the full-parent Lie certificate, `p2:thm:frc-automatic-compact-lie-closure` **1586–1664** requires an already supplied continuous faithful finite-dimensional affine-operation action on the actual closed group. The state-dual theorem says that **if an affine state map is supplied, its test dual is unique**. It does not produce the action or establish group faithfulness/continuity for all `Adm_R`. Hence existing issue **`BH-IIA-PORT-001`** remains exactly one issue; these four checked claims do **not** inherit it. No new foundational postulate or physical role occurrence is established or demanded by this batch.

**Conditional role status:** `p2:cor:generated-state-test-role-no-duplicate-witness` proves a canonical **co-realized role** when a G0-occurring structured role already generated a finite algebra in its physical test slot. It does **not** prove that every actual geometry generates such a test algebra, nor that all states are physically preparable, nor that a probability/frequency readout token occurs. The conditional is in the source statement; failure to prove its antecedent *universally* is not a missing step in this corollary.

## 45. Generated least-carrier theorem: verify its conditional, not stronger, assertion

Theorem `p2a:thm:generated-least-carrier` **3798–3836** assumes a **generated** complete role carrier `Car_X(S)` and, for each complete admissible `Y`, a **generated role-faithful** comparison `Car_X(S) \preceq_R Y`. It separately assumes that any two carriers with this universal property are structurally isomorphic. The GP3 definition **3714–3769** makes these conditions exactly a unique least class in the *role-faithful structured preorder*; closure under GP2 and lack of any competing equivalence-class selector prove retention. The source expressly abstains when no such carrier exists or additional member/history data are needed. **E1 verified, E3 verified by direct substitution into the actual preorder.** This theorem is not an unconditional creator of every imaginable downstream carrier and does not compare minimum complex dimensions.

This is the only GP3 step used in Batch 8: a mathematically least algebra generated inside `A` is promoted to a retained physical carrier *only if inclusion is an already licensed, role-faithful structural comparison*. Neither algebraic minimality nor mathematical availability alone is a new constructor.

## 46. Finite least invariant test algebra: independent E3 proof

For a licensed finite-dimensional complex unital `*`-algebra `A`, let `B_0=alg*(I,T)` and recursively

`B_{n+1}=alg*(B_n \cup \bigcup_a L_a(B_n))`.

Each `B_n` is a unital `*`-subalgebra and a complex linear subspace; `B_n \subseteq B_{n+1}`. If inclusion is strict, `dim_C B_{n+1} \ge dim_C B_n+1`. Thus **at most `dim_C A-dim_C B_0` strict increases** are possible, after which some `B_N=B_{N+1}`. The fixed-point identity implies `L_a(B_N) \subseteq B_N` **for every owned `a`**, and induction then gives `B_{N+k}=B_N`. For any invariant unital `*`-subalgebra `C` containing all initial tests, `B_0 \subseteq C` and, by induction, `B_n \subseteq C` for all `n`; hence `B_* \subseteq C`. Its leastness and uniqueness are **literal inclusion inside the fixed `A`**. Under the source's separate role-faithful-comparison antecedent the least-carrier theorem makes that algebra the retained GP3 carrier, but does not invent a missing physical semigroup or state.

The proof uses no assumption that a generator is inner, Lindblad, CP, Hamiltonian-selected, or that the family is finite/countable. If the complete owned family varies with time, it remains the family indexed by all of its *actually owned* generators. Closure under additional unowned generators would change the source claim and is not licensed.

**Verdict: CLOSED WITHIN CLAIMED SCOPE.** There is no missing functional-analytic limit: finite-dimensional strict stabilization is exact.

## 47. Surjective state restriction and descended semigroup: independent E3 proof

Let `B=B_* \subseteq A` be a unital finite-dimensional `C*`-subalgebra. The restriction map `q:S(A) -> S(B)` is affine, continuous and has a compact convex image `K`.

For any `eta \in S(B) \setminus K`, finite-dimensional strict affine separation and the already verified identification `B_sa \simeq Aff(S(B),R)` yield a self-adjoint `b\in B` with

`eta(b) > sup_{omega\in S(A)} omega(b) = lambda_max^A(b)`.

But `lambda_max^A(b) I-b` is a **positive element of B** (a unital subalgebra inherits the order and spectrum), so `eta(b) \le lambda_max^A(b)`, a contradiction. Therefore **q is onto**. This is a state-extension theorem; it does not select a unique extension. The original proof at **3907–3912** supplies the same argument and was independently checked.

For each `t>=0`, generator invariance gives `T_t(B) \subseteq B` because `T_t=exp(tL)` and `B` is finite-dimensional and `L`-invariant. The restricted `T_t|_B` is positive/unital and obeys the original semigroup law. Therefore

`bar F_t(eta) = eta \circ (T_t|_B)`

is an affine state semigroup on **all** `S(B)`, and

`q(F_t omega) = bar F_t(q omega)`.

The surjectivity of `q` makes its descended map unique on the entire target, rather than only on a proper image. Norm continuity of `t -> exp(tL)` and finite-dimensional evaluation prove joint continuity of `(t,eta) -> bar F_t eta`. No CP/associative automorphism or negative-time map is assumed.

**Independent equivalence challenge (E3, not an added theorem/physical operation).** For any unital `C*` subalgebra `C \subseteq A` and positive-unital map `T`, fiberwise projectability of `omega -> omega\circ T` through `q_C` is **equivalent** to `T(C)\subseteq C`. Forward: if projectable, for each `b\in C_sa`, `omega -> omega(Tb)` descends to an affine function of `q_C(omega)`; the state/test duality of **975–1057** represents this descended affine function as evaluation of a unique `c_b\in C_sa`. State separation on `A` then gives `Tb=c_b\in C`. Extend by complex linearity. Reverse: invariance directly defines the descended map. For `T_t=exp(tL)`, projectability for every `t>=0` is equivalent to `L(C)\subseteq C` (differentiate at `t=0` and exponentiate). Hence the source's least `B_*` is exactly the **least admissible algebra of owned tests admitting state-process descent**, provided the declared algebra and restriction words are licensed. This verifies the precise need for the closure hypothesis; it is not a new framework postulate or assertion of a physically realized process.

**Verdict: CLOSED WITHIN CLAIMED SCOPE.** The target is the state space of an existing *generated invariant test algebra*, not an arbitrary readout codomain.

## 48. Minimal linear observation space: E3 proof and factorization direction

For one owned positive-unital semigroup, `L(1)=0` and `L` preserves `*`. With initial Hermitian tests `E_i`, define `W_0=span_C{1,E_i}`, `W_{n+1}=W_n+L(W_n)`. The ascending sequence stabilizes in finite dimension. Its union is precisely the Krylov span `span_C{1,L^k E_i : k>=0}`. Let `O=span_C{1,T_t E_i:t>=0,i}`. Since the stabilized `W_*` is `L`-invariant, `O \subseteq W_*`; since finite-dimensional `O` is closed and every `L^k E_i` is the `k`th right derivative at `t=0` of `T_t E_i`, `W_* \subseteq O`. Thus `W_*=O` and the source's time-orbit identity **3930** is correct.

`W_*` is a unital self-adjoint operator space. Let `S(W_*)` mean its normalized Hermitian positive functionals for the **inherited order** `W_*\cap A_+`, not an algebra state space unless product closure was declared. The same compact-image/separation argument proves ambient-state restriction onto `S(W_*)`: if a positive `eta` is excluded, a separating self-adjoint `b\in W_*` contradicts positivity of `lambda_max^A(b)I-b\in W_*\cap A_+`. Two ambient states are equal on `W_*` iff they agree on all future `omega(T_t E_i)` expectations.

**Factorization orientation:** If a readout `r:S(A) -> Y` determines all these future expectations, then each fiber of `r` lies inside a fiber of `q_W`, so there is a unique map `h:r(S(A)) -> S(W_*)` with **`q_W = h \circ r`**. This is the precise direction of the source's wording “every other readout determining those expectations factors to this restriction on its actual image” (**3934–3935**). The reverse `r=h'\circ q_W` is NOT implied, since `r` may contain extra information; no reverse implication was used in the inspected proof. Its own image (rather than an unattained larger codomain) is essential.

If `L b_j = \sum_k M_{kj} b_k` in a basis of `W_*`, then `d omega_t(b_j)/dt = omega_t(L b_j)=\sum_k M_{kj} y_k`. In column notation `\dot y=M^T y`, verifying the printed orientation **3936–3937**. The source correctly qualifies physical operator-system retention by its licensed output sort and says product closure is a separate role (**3946–3953**).

**Verdict: CLOSED WITHIN CLAIMED SCOPE.** No unowned product test, full Hamiltonian or selected state has been smuggled into the linear target.

## 49. Adversarial E4: exact Pauli/two-qubit calculation

The independent exact check `Proofs/Paper2A-ObservableProcessBatch8-v1_1-verify.py` uses `A=M_2(C)\otimes M_2(C)`, `H=\sigma_x\otimes I`, `L(b)=i[H,b]`, and declared `E=\sigma_z\otimes I`. Then

- `B_0=span_C{I_4,\sigma_z\otimes I}` has dimension **2**.
- `L(\sigma_z\otimes I)=2\sigma_y\otimes I`, so `B_1=alg*(I_4,\sigma_z\otimes I,\sigma_y\otimes I)=M_2(C)\otimes I` has dimension **4**; `B_2=B_1`.
- The **linear** target is `W_*=span_C{I_4,\sigma_y\otimes I,\sigma_z\otimes I}`, dimension **3**, since `L(\sigma_y\otimes I)=-2\sigma_z\otimes I`. It is not product-closed, because `\sigma_y\sigma_z=i\sigma_x`.
- For every qubit state `rho` the two distinct ambient states `rho\otimes|0><0|` and `rho\otimes|1><1|` agree on all `B_*` tests. The extension `rho\otimes tau` shows onto restriction constructively in this example; unitary evolution `U_t=e^{-it\sigma_x}\otimes I` commutes with target restriction and gives a unique descended qubit evolution.
- **Invariance is indispensable:** on the noninvariant `B_0`, the two states `rho_\pm=(I\pm\sigma_y)/2` are indistinguishable on initial `I,Z` tests, but at `t=\pi/8` their future `Z` expectations differ by exactly `\sqrt{2}`, because `T_t(Z)=\cos(2t)Z+\sin(2t)Y`. Thus `q_{B_0}` **cannot** carry a descended state semigroup. This does NOT refute the source proposition, which explicitly requires `B_*` to be invariant; it is a sharp E4 validation of that hypothesis.

The Python exact-check results report algebra dimension 16, initial-algebra dimension 2, invariant-algebra dimension 4, minimal linear-space dimension 3, and the noninvariant descent failure `sqrt(2)`. Six groups of assertions passed. Finite examples are E4 controls, **not** the general proofs supplied in §§45–48.
