**Core derivation:** The constructor is licensed to provide `S_*^∂` on `J_∂`, and the independently typed `ι_P` gives a legitimate restriction `K_P=ι_P^*K_∂ι_P` of the response bilinear form on `J_P`. Promotion of the *whole* boundary energy or stress to ordinary-particle ownership is a different cross-sort statement. Because this output/identification is neither an already declared consequence of the restriction nor a generated relation in the theorem's premises, GP2 no insertion forbids silently adding it. This establishes the **negative implication** without denying that an independently generated stronger theorem can later prove the equality.

**Specialized no-splitting caution:** The original same-role lemma (`p2:lem:gp2-same-role-no-splitting`, 3196–3235) treats finite internal object images. It should not be applied as a universal theorem deleting all dynamical action modes. Here the source's negative owner-identification claim follows directly from GP2 no-insertion, typed-output scope and the stated independent owner guards.


## 142. E3/E4 independent mathematical controls

**Response restriction does not determine complete stress.** Choose `J_∂=R²`, `J_P=span(e₁)`, `K_∂=diag(2,5)`, `ι_P(t)=(t,0)`. Then `K_P=ι_P^T K_∂ι_P=2`, yet `K_∂≠diag(2,0)` and the complementary tangent has nonzero response `5`. Even two full responses `diag(2,5)` and `diag(2,17)` have the same particle restriction. This proves that response support on a physical particle carrier does not, by itself, determine the entire boundary response, let alone its stress tensor.

**Hessian equality does not imply stress equality.** Let `g` be a metric variable, `η` a cell variable, and `q` a field coordinate. The two finite local actions

`S_part(q;g,η)=(g+η)q²/2`,  `S_∂(q;g,η)=S_part(q;g,η)+c(g,η)`,  `c(g,η)=g²+3η`

have identical `q`-Hessians and identical first `q`-derivatives on the entire chart, yet `∂_g(S_∂−S_part)=2g` and `∂_η(S_∂−S_part)=3`. Any complete stress or work defined by these variations can differ. An added affine term `ℓ(g,η)q` is also invisible to the `q`-Hessian but is detected by the first field jet. Conversely, if `c` is constant in `g,η`, those additional derivatives vanish. This directly checks VI-A D19's mathematical certificate: same Hessian + first field jet leaves `c(g,η)`, and the stronger metric/cell stress identity requires its variation to vanish.

**Moving projectors have mixed jets.** If `K=diag(2,5)` and `ι(θ)=(cos θ,sin θ)`, then `K_P(θ)=2cos²θ+5sin²θ` and `∂_θK_P=3sin2θ`; at `θ=π/4` the derivative is `3` despite `∂_θK=0`. Correct differentiation requires `∂_θι^* Kι+ι^*K∂_θι` in addition to `ι^*(∂_θ K)ι`. Therefore an inherited response carrier alone does not certify off-shell metric/cell stress equality when the inclusion/projector moves.

**Fixed-owner scalar cell.** For `S_∂^(2)[q;ρ]=η(ρ) S_0(q)`, an already declared fixed owner of `q` is carried through every nonzero response in the same constructor. If `η=0`, there is no nonzero response from which to infer a physical owner. Inserting an independent multiplier `ξ` changes the frozen scalar response unless a separate deformation or normalization relation has been licensed. This is the exact scope of `v3108`, not a new physical efficiency prediction.

The companion executable `Proofs/Paper2A-BoundaryOwnerBatch24-v1_1-verify.py` performed **29/29 passing exact SymPy controls**. These are *counter-implication tests* and mathematical replay, not certificates that a particular physical particle stress tensor is realized.

Its `p8:cor:v2136-pb16-closed` (9499–9529) distinguishes owner closure from the **conditional** claim `Q_part^direct=Q_∂` and `ρ_part^direct(16)=ρ_∂,16`: those equalities consume action-jet data. Do not quote the older historical status as current owner status, or quote the later owner closure as unconditional stress closure.

# Batch 25 — Generated-member / owner-coordinate handoff (9 November 2026)

## 145. Frozen target, exact claim and status

**Current authoritative source:** `Paper2A-GeometricConstitution-v3_206.tex`, framework `Paper00-PapersMinimal-v2_358`, 12,149 lines, SHA-256 `167aec2ef3b1c3f30859ca65cf430778440887466a4451d47e6702af0edb9be0`. Audit source unchanged from the prior batch.

**Target theorem:** `p2a:thm:v3109-generated-member-owner-corealization` (11102–11147). If an occurring root `P` produces a **result-independent, frozen, nonredundant, licensed** tuple `Q=(q_1,...,q_N)`; an independently occurring physical owner `O` already has a declared typed insertion `iota_O:Q->T`; and **on the relevant joint constructor domain** its image is a required nonredundant output of this same physical owner, then `Occ(P) AND Occ(O) -> CoReal_T(iota_O(Q)|O)`. The already generated constituents cannot be independently deleted, renormalized, retyped or rebound while claiming the unchanged frozen presentation. This is **not** a claim that the mere existence of `P`, `O`, or a mathematical map creates the insertion or the physical owner.

**Status:** **VERIFIED-CONDITIONAL**, at the exact stated joint licensed/GP1-GP2-realizable constructor interface. No additional physical occurrence token, member selection, or GP4 is used.

**Related corollary:** `p2a:cor:v3109-finite-pair-cell-specialization` (11149–11170). An occurring finite quadratic owner with *predeclared* fermionic pair coordinate, and an occurring rooted constructor supplying the specific nonzero `Q_Delta=(Delta*,Delta*†)` in that coordinate, co-realize the exact Hermitian pairing cell and its fixed action. The source does not claim that any arbitrary matrix is a physical fermionic pairing coefficient.

## 147. E3 — Direct finite mathematical reconstruction

Take a finite typed rooted output `Q=(2,3)` and an owner with *already declared* identity insertion into a 2-coordinate target. The generated required image is `(2,3)`. Deleting either constituent, changing one coefficient, or swapping target coordinates changes the frozen input or output presentation. GP2 exactness therefore preserves the declared tuple and typed insertion with no additional normalization mark.

For a distinct (still licensed) **noninjective** insertion `iota(q1,q2)=q1+q2`, tuples `(2,3)` and `(4,1)` give the same scalar image. Thus image-level equality need **not** determine constituent values or make one source tuple redundant: provenance remains in the frozen `Q`, while the owner consumes only the predeclared target. If a later physical claim requires reconstruction of constituents, it must import a separate faithfulness/observability certificate. The theorem does not claim such inversion.

**Joint-domain stress:** Let the finite root and finite owner individually occur, but the typed joint-incidence relation be empty. Then their marginal occurrences alone cannot activate a constructor conditional on that joint incidence. This is outside the theorem's admitted *joint constructor domain*, and demonstrates why no unconditional `Occ(P) AND Occ(O)` to arbitrary cross-owner binding is legitimate.

## 148. E4 — Exact fermionic CAR controls and pair-cell scope

For two fermionic modes obeying CAR, let `a_1,a_2` be the exact 4×4 Jordan–Wigner annihilation matrices and set

`H_pair(Delta) = (sum_{ij} a_i† Delta_ij a_j† + sum_{ij} a_i (Delta†)_ij a_j)/2`.

Then `H_pair(Delta)†=H_pair(Delta)`. Since `a_i† a_j† = -a_j† a_i†`, only the antisymmetric coefficient `(Delta-Delta^T)/2` contributes. In particular a nonzero *symmetric* matrix gives zero pairing operator, while `Delta = [[0,2],[-2,0]]` gives a nonzero Hermitian operator and a different fixed action under a factor-three rescaling.

This tests a real interpretation limit, **not** a counterexample to the paper's downstream physical pair theorem: Paper IV-B's directly citing `p4:cor:v4101-generated-pair-member-handoff` (5173–5212) explicitly requires `Delta* in Lambda² K*`, `Delta*^T=-Delta*`, `Delta*!=0`, and a licensed owner-preserving support map. That certificate rules out the symmetric-kernel toy model. Paper V-A (3504–3568) also posits a faithful support insertion for its specific rooted neutral cell; this audit **traces** that direct use, not a global proof of its physical K16 closure.

# Batch 28 — Finite visible response without a dark-complement selector (9 November 2026)

## 162. Exact theorem and E1 source freeze

**Paper II-A:** `p2a:cor:v227-visible-closure`, 11271–11284; immediately following scope clause 11285–11290. **Imported Paper I-B:** `p1b:thm:v2210-lit-finite-weyl` 18949–18988; `p1b:thm:response-minimal-visible-realization` 18995–19053; `p1b:thm:v227-finite-response` 22122–22158; `p1b:prop:v227-sharp-sample-count` 22160–22186; `p1b:cor:v227-response-jets` 22188–22213; `p1b:cor:v227-zero-mode-comparison` 22227–22240.

The actual theorem says that exact equality of two **complete** Hermitian response matrices at `m+1` distinct upper-half-plane energies suffices when both complementary dimensions are at most `m`, under a shared retained/source target and common kinetic/energy normalization. An already licensed pole/readout depending *only on that response* is consequently common without selecting an unused dark complementary block. It does not construct the actual parent, ports, physics action or detector.

## 163. E3 finite-polynomial proof and sharpness

Let `d_i(z)=det(C_i-zI)`, `N=m_1+m_2`, and `P(z)=d_1(z)d_2(z)(M_1(z)-M_2(z))`, where `M_i(z)=zI-A_i+B_i(C_i-zI)^(-1)B_i^*`. The `zI` terms cancel and `deg P <= N`; Hermiticity gives `P(conj z)^* = P(z)`. Each upper-half-plane exact zero supplies a lower-half-plane zero, hence `2 s > N` samples make `P` the zero matrix polynomial. The source formula `s=floor(N/2)+1` follows, with the safe bound `m+1` when `m_i<=m`. The lower bound is not an arbitrary numerical fit: the Paper I-B positive Hermitian sharp witness agrees at `i` and `2i`, differs at `3i` when `m_1=m_2=2`.

## 164. E3 visible/dark classification and conditional readout

The exact response fixes `A`, visible pole locations and positive coefficient matrices `R_lambda=BP_lambda B^*`; the minimal visible environment has complex dimension `sum rank R_lambda` and is unique up to a unitary fixing the retained frame. A reducing uncoupled dark complement is invisible *only to that response*: no selection of its internal eigenvalues is needed for response-only readouts. Other source ports, heat/action traces, absolute gravity, metric jets and physical owner may inspect it and require independent certified information.

## 165. E3/E4 source-jet and adverse controls

For `C^k` real-source families on fixed finite carriers, sampled equality of source jets to order `k` at `s_k=floor((k+1)(m_1+m_2)/2)+1` fixed common upper-half-plane energies determines those jet functions of spectral energy **at that source point**; it is not global curvature/source equality. Upper-half-plane tests permit complementary zero modes, whereas coupled zero modes remain in real-zero calculations. Scalar contractions or partial native coefficient tests do not substitute for full retained/source matrix equality. A common frame and exact, not approximate, samples are required. The accompanying executable SymPy script independently passes **30/30** exact controls.

## B29-E3. Conditional positive proof

Let the exact frozen origin word construct the actual action and its insertion from jointly occurring input roles. If every certificate slot is satisfied, then (i) the preterminal typed relation is retained through the descended map by PCAD; (ii) the same action's complete cross returns are retained by the v3102 theorem, including the declared coefficient and contact terms; (iii) the already constituted owner supplies the variation/reduction domain and declared observable map; (iv) the existing generated-image rule gives the output as a descendant of that owner. GP2 does not require a new occurrence token for such an already-required descendant. **The conclusion is conditional on the five-slot certificate; none is generated by this reasoning.**

## B29-E3. Negative direction and adversarial examples

A nonzero provider in a nonzero vector space permits a **zero** linear insertion. In a finite two-dimensional control, `P=(1,2)` and `iota=0` give `iota(P)=0`. Thus the port's nonzero norm proves no nonzero source value or action insertion. An unchosen nonzero morphism may exist in the Hom-space simultaneously; mathematical availability does not certify that its morphism is licensed by the physical owner.

The matrices `A=diag(1,0)` and `B=diag(0,1)` have identical scalar trace 1, but distinct source matrix elements. Likewise the two-channel tuples `(1,1)` and `(1,-1)` have identical separate absolute-value readouts yet coherent returns 4 and 0. These are mathematical witnesses to the failure of terminal readout to determine a preterminal relation. They are **not claimed as counterexamples inside a GP2-admissible physical comparison class**.

For `S(x,z;g)=1/2(5+g)x²+2xz+3z²/2+g²`, stationarity gives `z*=-2x/3` and

\[
S_{\rm red}=\tfrac12(5+g-4/3)x^2+g^2,\qquad
\chi_{\rm red}=5+g-4/3.
\]

Omitting the mixed term would incorrectly give `5+g`; omitting the field-independent `g²` leaves the field Hessian unchanged but alters the metric/source variation by `2g`. That shows why action-level and readout-level agreement are different. For fermionic source normalization, `M=Z_L^{-1/2}(\delta^2 S/\delta\bar\psi_L\delta\psi_R)Z_R^{-1/2}` must retain orientation and the right word; the finite off-diagonal example gives unequal answers when left/right dressings are exchanged. These calculations **test mathematical validity only**, not physical action ownership.

## B29-E3. Adjacent observable-minimal result

The corollary at 11368–11391 has two **alternative** sufficient routes for licensed `r=tilde r∘F`:

- **Family route:** an *actual* generated output `X` is shown to lie in nonempty licensed family `𝔛`; `F` is constant on that family. Then `r(X)` is fixed without selecting `X`.
- **Direct action-invariant route:** the actual action contraction itself determines `F(X)`, even if a complete operator reconstruction is unavailable. Then apply `tilde r`.

The two routes are **OR**, not two required independent postulates. Both retain the action-origin premise: neither permits replacing a physical action by a geometrically available comparison family. These statements are direct consequences of `p2a:cor:v228-constant-source-fibre`, `p2a:thm:v3102-preterminal-relation-preservation` and factorization.

The polynomial certificate proposition at 11393–11413 gives two separately sufficient real-algebraic checks on an already licensed **nonempty** real family `C_j(D,z)=0` for a licensed polynomial observable `O`:

\[
 d^N\bigl(O-o(D)\bigr)=\sum_jh_j C_j\ (d\ne0),\quad\text{or}\quad
 \bigl(O-o(D)\bigr)^{2k}+\sum_\ell s_\ell^2=\sum_jh_jC_j.
\]

The ideal-membership route uses the **nonzero divisor** `d` on the admitted domain. The SOS route uses **real** nonnegativity; it does not hold over arbitrary complex variables. Neither proves that a polynomial certificate of bounded degree always exists. The proposition correctly retains all these conditions and does not license the readout or action.

The anchored heat theorem imports the quantitative Bregman theorem at its last sentence; the normalized determinant proposition independently imports the anchored row domain and Hadamard. `p2a:prop:v267-unmarked-heat-no-converse` is a **negative boundary control** for the rooted theorem, not a missing assumption. ### E3 independent proof: matrix heat–Bregman

For `X=B-D`, `H(u)=D+uX`, define `f(u)=Tr exp(-τH(u))`. Twice differentiating the trace yields

`f''(u)=τ²∫₀¹ Tr[X exp(-τ(1-v)H(u)) X exp(-τvH(u))] dv`.

In an orthonormal eigenbasis of `H(u)`, the integrand is `Σ_{ij}|X_ij|² exp(-τ[(1-v)h_i+v h_j])`; each coefficient is positive. Thus, if `X≠0`, `f''(u)>0` at every `u`, and Taylor's integral remainder gives `Hτ(B,D)=∫₀¹(1-u)f''(u)du>0`. If the two endpoints lie in the order interval `[0,LI]`, all eigenvalues of `H(u)` lie there and every weight lies in `[exp(-τL),1]`. This proves the two-sided HS bound without assuming simultaneous diagonalizability. For several sectors, a strictly positive weight sum of nonnegative gaps vanishes only sectorwise.

**E4 stress tests:** `B=[[1,1],[1,1]]`, `D=diag(2,0)` are distinct, noncommuting and isospectral, so all unmarked traces agree, but `H_{ln 2}(B,D)=(3/4)ln2>0`. The upper quadratic estimate is not global when the order-box premise is omitted: scalar `B=-1,D=0,τ=1` gives `e−2>1/2`. This is a *scope validation*, not a flaw in the source (which states the order box).

### E3 independent proof: tagged Euclidean frequency

For spectral measure `μ_g` of a normalized vector, `d=∫x dμ_g` and `Rω=∫1/(ω²+x)dμ_g`. Use the rational identity

`(d-x)/(ω²+x) = (d-x)/(ω²+d) + (d-x)²/[(ω²+d)(ω²+x)]`.

Integrating eliminates the first term and yields the stated positive gap. Because `(ω²+B)^{-1}>0`, equality iff `(B-d)g=0`. On the cyclic tagged vector this gives `〈g,(z²−B)^{-1}g〉=1/(z²−d)` for all nonpole `z`. No claim is made about the other left directions or right-sector coupling. Example `B=diag(2,10)` and `B'=diag(2,100)` have the same complete `g=e₁` tagged response and different full matrices. This tagged free calculation is not an interacting pole certificate.

### E3 independent proof: rooted heat and determinant

Given a complete rooted ON left basis, `D=Σλ_r|g_r〉〈g_r|` and independent moments `〈g_r,Bg_r〉=λ_r`, scalar Jensen for the spectral measure of each `g_r` gives `〈g_r,e^(−τB)g_r〉≥exp(−τλ_r)`; strictness forces `Bg_r=λ_r g_r`. Summation over a *complete* ON frame makes equality iff `B=D`. The linear Bregman correction vanishes because each rooted diagonal element of `B-D` vanishes, so the quantitative Bregman bound transfers exactly. For an anchored rank-one-row packet `M=Σ√λ_r g_r r_r†`, with `||r_r||=1`, `M†g_r=√λ_r r_r`, verifying the first moments. The Gram `G_{rs}=〈r_r,r_s〉` has unit diagonal; `B=Λ^{1/2}GΛ^{1/2}`; Hadamard gives `det B≤det Λ`, equality iff all right rays are orthonormal. Positive `λ_r` matters for this iff.

Example `M=[[1,0],[6/5,8/5]]` yields `B=[[1,6/5],[6/5,4]]`, with `D=diag(1,4)`, exact moments and `det B=64/25<4`; rooted heat difference is strictly positive. Completeness cannot be dropped: on an incomplete rooted frame, untested block(s) can vary without changing the marked observations.

### E3 independent proof: exact relation-fibre criterion

For real polynomial constraints `C_j(x)=0`, the ideal `J=<C(x),C(y),q(x)-q(y)>` defines exactly the same-data pairs. `O` factors through `q` as a set map on `q(F)` iff `O_i(x)-O_i(y)` vanishes on all real zeros of `J`. By the **real** Nullstellensatz, this is equivalent to membership in `sqrt_R(J)` for each component. It is not the complex radical and does not assert that the descended set map is polynomial or analytic. For `q=x²`, `O=x⁴` factors (difference divisible by `x²-y²`); `O=x` does not (compare `x=1` and `y=-1`).

### E4 negative unmarked trace example and direct consumer

Take `D=diag(1,2,4)` and an orthogonal `L` rotating the 1st and 3rd eigenvectors with `sin φ=4/5`. Then `B=LDL†≠D` shares **every** unmarked heat trace with `D` but `Tr(D²)-Tr(DB)=144/25`. Thus a single unmarked heat value alone never proves rooted alignment on the general domain; the source expressly contains this counter-boundary.

The scalar Euclidean heat extraction at 11881–11908 explicitly demands the same physical finite chiral action source, normalization and removal of extra contributions. No other external paper proof is declared fully audited by this Batch 30.

## 3. Independent E3 group proof

Set `G=A_5`, acting on `Ω={1,2,3,4,5}`. The subgroup `A_{4,i}={g∈A_5:g(i)=i}` has order `60/5=12`. The five stabilizers are distinct and conjugate. For `i≠j`, fixing both labels permits only even permutations of the remaining three labels, hence `A_{4,i}∩A_{4,j}≅C_3`, order three. Every triple stabilizer intersects in the identity. The inverse image under `2I→A_5` of each order-three subgroup has order six and is cyclic: any central `C_2` extension of `C_3` is cyclic of order six. Thus `H_i≅2T`, `H_i∩H_j≅C_6`, and the ten axes are distinct, with pairwise intersection precisely the center.

The ten unordered two-subsets of `Ω` are the ten Petersen vertices. Two vertices are adjacent iff their two-label subsets are disjoint. Each has `C(3,2)=3` neighbors; there are `10×3/2=15` edges. `A_5` acts transitively on points, two-subsets, and unordered disjoint two-subset pairs. Their stabilizer orders are respectively `60/5=12`, `60/10=6`, and `60/15=4`. The kernel of the `2I` action is the common central `C_2`, as asserted.

A `2T` support has exactly one subgroup of order 24 (itself). The projective `S_4` of `2O` has only one `A_4` subgroup, because any subgroup of order 12 is index two and hence normal, and the sign homomorphism gives its unique index-two subgroup. The quotient `A_5` of `2I` has five `A_4` subgroups, namely the point stabilizers. Thus among already-listed candidate types `[2T],[2O],[2I]`, **only `[2I]`** meets the independently frozen five-core incidence. Nothing here produces the requirement or the physical occurrence of that candidate.

## 4. Independent E4 exact certificate

The executable `Proofs/Paper2A-PetersenBatch31-v1_1-verify.py` enumerates `A_5` from parity-even permutations, point-stabilizer cosets, pair intersections, 10-vertex graph adjacency and 15 edges. It verifies the action orbits and stabilizer sizes, the exact Petersen matrix identity `A²=2I−A+J` and spectrum `(3,1^5,−2^4)`, the core incidence map rank 5 and quadrupole projector rank 5, and the graph Hodge edge-cycle dimensions 9+6=15. It separately reconstructs the ten **published** `Q(√5)` projective axis coordinates of Paper I-A 3757–3769, their two exact squared overlaps `5/9` and `1/9`, the tight four-axis core frames and rank-five quadrupole Gram `G=(4/3)P_5`. The group-classification boundary and nonconverse are also checked. **39/39 named exact checks passed**. Arithmetic is exact (`sympy`, Python integer/permutation enumeration); no numerical tolerance or fitted physics is used. The script and full named results are included in the successor archive.

## 5. Adversarial boundary checks

- **Unconditional-support trap:** a generic `S^3`, an existing `SU(2)` parent, or the 15-dimensional `H_1` seed does not by itself generate a finite embedded `2I` support. The theorem assumes `Supp_{2I}` has already occurred. This is not a missing premise in a conditional theorem.
- **Circular five-core selector trap:** declaring the required five-core relation *because* `2I` happens to support it would not meet the frozen-role premise of the corollary; that route is excluded by the source itself (11647–11650).
- **Abstract-vs-physical 15 trap:** fifteen Petersen edges, fifteen harmonic components, fifteen group-induced operators, and fifteen physical particles are distinct sorts; no type identification is proved by dimension alone.
- **Mark/effect trap:** the whole unmarked orbit is mathematically generated; no one `H_i`, `C_6` sign, edge orientation, preparation or effect becomes a G0-occurring token.
- **Aut(Petersen) trap:** the full abstract relabeling group is `S_5`, but the *inner* action from the supplied `2I` factors only through `A_5`.
- **External physics trap:** the finite module proof does not establish a gauge coupling, mass coefficient, particle spectrum, or measured probability.

## 3. Independent mathematical replay (E3)

Put `t_i=-iσ_i/2` in `su(2)`, with `[t_i,t_j]=ε_{ijk}t_k`. The adjoint Killing form in this basis is `B(t_i,t_j)=-2δ_ij`. A bi-invariant metric on a round embedded radius-`R` three-sphere satisfies `g_R(t_i,t_j)=R²δ_ij/4=-(R²/8)B(t_i,t_j)`. Hence the metric is indeed a single positive Killing ray. The invariant symmetric bilinear form equations `ad(t_i)^T G+G ad(t_i)=0` have a one-dimensional solution space `G=aI`, `a>0`; there are no additional isotropy-invariant shape moduli.

For `e_i=2t_i/R`, the orthonormal-frame brackets are `[e_i,e_j]=(2/R)ε_{ijk}e_k`. The Levi-Civita connection of the bi-invariant metric is `∇_{e_i}e_j = 1/2[e_i,e_j]`. Therefore every orthonormal two-plane has sectional curvature

`K(e_i,e_j)=(1/4)||[e_i,e_j]||²=1/R²`, `i≠j`.

Since the dimension is three, `Scal=2∑_{i<j}K(e_i,e_j)=6/R²` and `Ric=(2/R²)g`. Under `R→λR`, `Scal→λ^{-2}Scal`, so `K_p R²=6` is invariant. The scalar-curvature length is `ell_K=K_p^{-1/2}=R/√6`; it is **not** numerically the sphere radius. This proves the corollary's mathematical content independently of its source table.

There is no radius selector in the result: the map `R∈(0,∞)↔K_p=6/R²∈(0,∞)` is one-to-one, and the whole positive ray remains. The source's statement that *a later generated dimensionful curvature standard may actualize* the ray is explicitly conditional, not itself a G0/GP selector.

## B33-2. Exact geometric verification

Let `M` be a smooth manifold. For `X∈Γ(TM)`, define `δ_X f=X(f)`. Then the differential operator obeys the Leibniz identity `δ_X(fg)=δ_X(f)g+fδ_X(g)`, and the vector-field commutator obeys `[δ_X,δ_Y]=δ_[X,Y]`. For finite-dimensional smooth manifolds, local coordinate functions separate vector fields, so `δ` is injective as a mathematical map into derivations. **None** of these mathematical statements implies that the codomain action is already generated as a *physical* operation: the source requires a frozen typed action constructor.

The momentum-seed example in the source has stronger, checkable content. On the round `S³(R)⊂R⁴`, let `x_i` be the degree-one coordinate harmonic and `V_i=∇x_i` the intrinsic gradient. In ambient coordinates `V_i=∂_i-(x_i/R²)∑_k x_k ∂_k` along the sphere. Therefore

`[V_i,V_j]=(x_i ∂_j-x_j ∂_i)/R²`.

These six brackets for `i<j` are linearly independent global Killing sections and span the six-dimensional `so(4)` action. Thus the generated vector-field image is finite-dimensional **as a mathematical subspace**, whereas `Γ(TS³)` remains infinite-dimensional. The E4 certificate verifies all `6×4=24` bracket coordinate identities, six induced derivation identities, the six-section rank, and the Leibniz rules. This verifies the *mathematical feasibility of* finite generated images without equating them to the entire smooth parent carrier or certifying physical actuality.

An independent `S¹` check takes `X=sinθ∂θ`, `Y=cosθ∂θ`, giving `[X,Y]=-∂θ`. It independently verifies the induced derivation commutator identity and product rule.

### 1.1 Definitional precision: atomicity is not primitive physical occurrence

For a frozen commutative type monoid `M_Σ`, `a` is atomic when `a≠0` and every decomposition `a=b+c` has a zero summand. This is a type predicate on the *already generated* monoid. It cannot declare a new physical root, infer smallest dimension, or classify an after-the-fact chosen representation as primitive. For the free commutative monoid `N²` with generators `e₁,e₂`, `e₁` and `e₂` are atoms, while `e₁+e₂` is decomposable. Constructor length `ℓ(i,j)=i+j` gives finite sublevels of cardinality `(N+1)(N+2)/2`, invariant under the unmarked swap `e₁↔e₂`. These are existence **examples**, not a framework selection of `N`.

### 1.2 Capacity, occupation and family bounds are distinct GP1 clauses

A one-dimensional one-particle space still has `dim Symⁿ(C)=1` for every `n`, hence `⊕_{n≥0} Symⁿ(C)` is infinite-dimensional without a constituted occupation cutoff. GP1 explicitly requires finite *total retained* composition/occupation capacity; it does not infer it from one-particle finiteness.

Likewise the family `H_n=nH`, for a fixed nonzero `2×2` Hermitian `H`, is unbounded in operation norm even though every `H_n` acts on the same finite carrier. Under GP1 FRC-b it is inadmissible **if the full unbounded family is constituted as a physical datum**; finitely many chosen operations or a symbolic family have different typed scope. FRC-b is not a universal bound on all mathematically expressible multiples of `H`.

A GP1 obstruction is a negative admissibility judgment. The source's GP2 generated-output and Paper III-A continuation requirements are separately needed for any *positive* successor process. Nothing in the target remark asserts that a violating candidate undergoes a physically occurring transition.

## 2. Backward proof slice and actual edges

- **Frozen role/constructor typing:** earlier `p2:def:finite-presentation-convention` (856–869), `p2:def:structured-role-realization` (871–928), plus the source's GP2 generated-host/no-insertion and no-omission clauses (2877–2919) justify why only prior licensed constructor schemata enter physical generation.

- **Registered boundary:** `p2:def:intrinsic-generation-grammar` (1416–1443) is necessary to *define* `P_gbd(q,N)` (1517–1533); a physical stage must additionally license/register the boundary. `p2:prop:gp1-gp2-bounded-generated-exactness` (3601–3638) then makes the generated exact closure relative to the previously registered sublevel, not to an arbitrary postselected cut. The source's later statement at 7220–7245 makes clear that this does not select a specific `N`.
- **Finite comparison bridge:** finite `C*` state-space conventions (`p2:def:standard-finite-state-space`, 930–959) and finite-dimensional state duality (`p2:thm:canonical-finite-state-test-evaluation`, 975–1057; already audited in Batch 7) give a compact state space. `p2:cor:gp1-tb` (1547–1585) *also* consumes the explicitly assumed uniform modulus / bounded test family. The later compact-isometry theorem (7130 onward) uses this typed TB bridge, but TB is not itself a Lie-certificate.
- **Separate faithful affine/Lie port:** `p2:thm:frc-automatic-compact-lie-closure` (1587–1665) requires an additional faithful state-affine group action, already tracked under `BH-IIA-PORT-001`. This batch does **not** discharge that all-full-parent port.

## 3. Independent E3 proof — GP1-TB

Let `A` be a finite-dimensional unital complex `C*` algebra, `S(A)` its state space, and `ι:X→S(A)` an injective realized-state map. The set `ι(X)` is totally bounded in any finite-dimensional state norm, even when it is not closed. Assume for a comparison metric `d` on `X` that

`d(x,y) ≤ ω(‖ι(x)−ι(y)‖*)` with `ω(t)→0` as `t→0`.

Fix `ε>0` and choose `δ>0` such that `ω(t)<ε` whenever `0≤t<δ`. A finite `δ/2`-net in `ι(X)` can be centered in `ι(X)` itself; any two points associated to the same center are at norm distance less than `δ`, so their `d` distance is less than `ε`. This yields a finite `ε`-cover of `(X,d)` and hence a finite maximal size for every `ε`-separated set. If `d` is induced by a uniformly operator-norm-bounded test family `T`, then

`d(x,y)=sup_{a∈T}|(ι(x)−ι(y))(a)| ≤ (sup_{a∈T}‖a‖) ‖ι(x)−ι(y)‖*`, 

so the modulus holds with `ω(t)=Mt`. No finite cardinality, minimum positive separation, unique radius, or Lie action follows.

**Necessary-hypothesis adversarial control:** take infinitely many distinct rational `p∈(0,1)`, embed faithfully as `ρ_p=diag(p,1−p)` in `S(M₂(C))`, but impose an unrelated discrete comparison `d(p,q)=1` whenever `p≠q`. This comparison is not totally bounded and has **no uniform state-norm comparison modulus**. It is outside `p2:cor:gp1-tb`'s hypotheses, not an admissible counterexample to that theorem. Merely having a finite state algebra never supplies the required bridge.

## 4. Independent E3 boundary analysis

The condition `P_gbd(q,N)` **alone** is a support-inclusion statement, not a claim that the realized support literally equals `Γ_q^{≤N}`. If a source consumer requires *all* nonredundant generated sublevel items, that stronger completeness follows only when the frozen grammar includes those items and GP2 exactness/no-omission applies, as in lines 3624–3637. The FRC-d remark is correct as a description of this **joint** boundary-and-exactification mechanism; the inclusion alone cannot carry the whole lower-ideal conclusion.

Every finite support of finite-length elements is below *some* finite `N` after a specific intrinsic grammar exists, but this existential mathematical observation does **not** certify a particular a priori physical boundary, the completeness of the retained sublevel, or candidate-independence of its constructor. The source preserves these distinctions. A chosen cutoff introduced only because it reproduces a desired physical number is not intrinsic under lines 1433–1438.

The bounded direct consumer scan found the following correct guarded uses:

- Paper II-A 5982–6000: intrinsic word-budget representation bookkeeping is not automatically the physical tensor-word grammar.

These bounded source checks are not a full independent audit of Papers III-F, IV-A or IV-B.

## Batch 35 — Qubit affine-lift obstruction and isometry/Lie firewall

# Paper II-A — Batch 35: Qubit affine-lift obstruction and Lie-port firewall

**Current source:** `Paper2A-GeometricConstitution-v3_206.tex`, Framework v2.358, SHA-256 `167aec2ef3b1c3f30859ca65cf430778440887466a4451d47e6702af0edb9be0`.  
**Audit scope:** source lines 1587–1709, with the proposition at 1667–1696 and the firewall remark at 1698–1709.  
**Verdict:** two newly reviewed source objects, both **VERIFIED at stated mathematical scope**. No new Paper II-A proof gap, four inherited proof issues remain unresolved. One inherited-atlas link defect found and repaired in the **new current index only**; historical bytes preserved.  
**Method:** one Chat-mode source replay, independently derived representation-theoretic check, exact symbolic adversarial controls. No independent Work agents; 29/29 exact checks passed, with an early test-harness normalization mismatch corrected before final replay.

## 1. Frozen claims and quantifiers

`p2a:prop:v281-qubit-affine-lift-obstruction` (1667–1696) asserts a **specific impossibility**: exact conjugation on the qubit full density-matrix state space has faithful compact affine operation group `PU(2)≅SO(3)` while that *same quotient group* has no faithful continuous 2-complex-dimensional linear representation on the original `H=C²`. It does **not** assert no faithful 3D complex or 4D real affine representation. It also does not refute linear action of its double cover `SU(2)`.

`p2a:rem:v281-isometry-affine-lie-firewall` (1698–1709) states that GP1-TB/compact recurrence alone give compact **topological** operation closure; faithful finite-dimensional state/test embedding does not automatically make metric isometries affine. Lie regularity requires a faithfully represented generated affine operation port, independent faithful linear representation, or Lie certificate.

Upstream `p2:thm:frc-automatic-compact-lie-closure` (1587–1665), already in the historical ledger, is **conditional**: finite faithful `j:Xhat→S(A)`, `j` continuous, and each forward exact operation with affine, role-faithful action on `V=aff_R(j(Xhat))`, preserving composition. The finite-frame argument extends to a continuous faithful homogeneous representation of the *closed parent*, proving compact Lie. A separate continuous faithful complex-linear action on `H` is required for a carrier lift. Its proof does not derive that lift.

`p2:thm:gp1-bounded-isometry-closure` (7129–7187) supplies the compact topological parent, with reversibility derived from the closure of forward isometries. This is not itself a faithful finite-dimensional linear action.

## 2. Backward proof slice and E1 replay

## 3. Independent representation-theoretic derivation

Let `σ_i` be the Pauli matrices. Every qubit state is `ρ(r)=1/2(I+r·σ)` with `|r|≤1`. For `U∈SU(2)`, conjugation `ρ→UρU†` implements a 3D rotation on the Bloch vector. Its kernel is the two-element center `{+I,-I}`: any matrix commuting with all `σ_i` is scalar, and `det U=1` restricts it to `±I`. Thus the faithful operation group is `SU(2)/{±I}=SO(3)`. It is faithful on the 3D real traceless Hermitian test space; the homogeneous affine action is 4D real.

Suppose a continuous complex 2D linear representation of `SO(3)` existed and were faithful. Pull back to `SU(2)`. Every finite-dimensional complex representation of compact `SU(2)` is completely reducible. In dimension 2, the only possibilities are two trivial one-dimensional factors or the nontrivial spin-1/2 irrep. The latter maps the central `-I` to `-I₂`, so it cannot factor through `SO(3)`. The former is trivial and not faithful. Contradiction. The SO(3) complex vector representation is 3D and faithful; no universal `dim(H)` bound on a carrier lift follows.

These are statements about **the exact operation quotient**. Replacing it with `SU(2)` changes the representation group and is not a carrier lift of the same quotient.

## 4. Connected compact non-affinity control (E4)

Equip `X=S¹` with its usual round metric and define the faithful state embedding `j(t)∈S(M₂(C))` through its Bloch vector

`r(t)=(cos t, sin t, cos2t)/4`.

The vector has norm ≤`sqrt(2)/4<1`, and the first two coordinates make the map injective. Compactness makes `j` a homeomorphism onto its image and the inverse uniformly continuous. The round-metric isometries include shifts `t→t+θ`.

The affine span of the image is spanned by functions `{1,cos t,sin t,cos2t}`. For `θ=π/4`, the shifted third coordinate is `cos(2t+π/2)=-sin2t`, which is linearly independent of that span. Thus there exists **no** affine `A` on the 3D state affine span satisfying `Aj(t)=j(t+π/4)` for all t. The eight-point exact evaluation matrix has rank 4; adjoining the translated third-coordinate values raises rank to 5. By contrast, a `π/2` shift of t preserves the span, demonstrating the specific nature of the obstruction.

The control refutes only the invalid inference `finite-state embedding + metric isometry ⇒ state-affine action`, **not** the primitive-role theorem: this example is not asserted to realize the complete GP2 primitive-role signature. It also has a compact Lie group of intrinsic isometries in spite of the non-affine embedding, proving that *failure of this port* does not imply that a group is non-Lie.

## 3. Independently derived mathematics (E3)

### Scalar and cardinality classification

For `f∈End_C(C)`, linearity forces `f(z)=zf(1)` and conversely every `a∈C` defines `f_a(z)=az`. In one complex dimension dagger is conjugation, positivity is `|w|²≥0`, `0≤z≤1` gives the effect interval, and unitarity is `|z|=1`. A further idempotence relation gives only `{0,1}`, demonstrating that the four display slices are not exhaustive *restrictions*.

For `n=dim_C H>0`, `End(H)^*≅C^{n²}` has cardinal `c`. A finite-parametrized finite diagram contributes at most `c` instances; countably many such diagrams remain ≤`c`. If `X` is nonempty separable, a continuous `f:X→C` is determined by its restriction to a countable dense subset: `|C(X,C)|≤c^{aleph_0}=c`; arbitrary constants give the lower bound. If `|X|=c`, all (not necessarily continuous) functions have cardinal `c^c=2^c`. These are cardinality statements, not measured-quantity predictions.

### Canonical duality versus arbitrary natural functional

The coevaluation is the identity tensor, so it is basis independent, and the unmarked contraction is `Tr` exactly. The isomorphism `(End H)^*≅End H` via the trace pairing is proved using matrix units. A rank-one mark `B=ψ⊗ε` gives `Tr(BT)=ε(Tψ)`. The same complex number can therefore arise from an unmarked loop or a marked boundary process. **Disjointness resides in the frozen *diagram provenance*, not the numeric codomain C.** This is already the distinction in lines 1961–1963 and 1981–1985, so it is not a new gap.

The family `λ_H(T)=Tr(T)/dim H` is unitary natural, tensor multiplicative and normalized at `H=C`, but fails unnormalized direct-sum additivity. Thus it does not refute the theorem, whose antecedent is the particular canonical duality word. The separate II-F coefficient lemma correctly classifies the broader family and the need for licensed sum links.

### Two types, not two functions

The normal-form theorem divides **primitive connected typed diagram occurrences** into external-boundary empty and nonempty. Internal loops within a boundary process remain generated in its effective arrow `T`; a numerically equal loop rewriting with a rank-one insertion does not erase the boundary provenance. Unlicensed tensor/sum/exterior/oriented/entropy/function-calculus decorations cannot be admitted by ambient category mathematics alone. The result does **not** prove that every physically possible observable is a realized member of `FSR_Σ`, or that the two role types fix a unique readout law or value.

A first test-harness false failure from unevaluated SymPy conjugation was corrected by explicit exact symbolic expansion before the passing replay. **The tests are representative algebraic checks, not an exhaustive enumeration of all finite diagrams or proof of uncountable cardinal arithmetic.** E3 proofs supply those general results.

## B. Backward proof slices and the earliest necessary edges

### B1. Constituents of one occurring composite

`p2:prin:geometric-primacy-minimal-constitution` (1243–1366) provides the claim-relative G0 occurrence; the finite presentation definition (2017–2043) freezes the constructor `G_Σ`, its full list of required nonredundant constituents, and each native sort; GP2's `no omission` and `typed host faithfulness` clauses (2906–2929) force their correct simultaneous representation. The no-insertion clause (2895–2904) and the final proof sentences (2075–2080) prohibit claiming an apparatus or independent scalar estimator as an unlicensed extra constituent.

Independent finite-content proof: let `R={(port_i, sort_i, value_i)}` be the finite set of frozen nonredundant required typed constituents and let `Pres(M)` be the exact realization's represented typed set. GP2 gives `R ⊆ Pres(M)` with no deletion or retyping, and no inserted marked content. Therefore every `port_i` occurs in the correct sort, irrespective of numerical coincidences between different sorts. The implication is about *represented generated content*, not an `Actual_E(r_i)` primitive-token predicate. This is not circular: the definition supplies generator and required-output **status**, whereas the theorem supplies faithful *representation in the occurring composite*.

The mathematically stronger statement “every diagram that could be made in Σ occurs physically” is false and not claimed. For a G0-occurring P with only frozen `(object, operation)` ports, a new apparatus diagram or history is not a constituent solely because that diagram is in the ambient mathematical grammar.

### B2. Generated-role content

The role-descendant definition 2083–2102 requires more than a finite list: an exact generated role, frozen law/topology and GP1 structured realizability. The existing theorem 2104–2136 co-realizes that *role*; neither theorem implies actualization of all its values, preparations or measurements. An autonomous additional carrier is not silently inferred; `p2:lem:gp2-same-role-no-splitting` (3196–3235) is needed when a specific generated finite object is to be internal to an already constituted carrier.

### B3. Constructor origin and the explicit obstruction branch

The origin certificate 2197–2230 provides precisely a finite typed path whose inputs, intermediate outputs and target sort were fixed independently of result. Given the roots `RootReal_P(D)` of 2235–2240, every intermediate step and final `q` belong to `Gen_Σ(D)`. If there is no GP1-finite GP2-exact faithful candidate, `Obs_FRC` is the defined obstruction and no physical q is asserted. Otherwise, for every admissible candidate, omission/retyping of q violates GP2 (2260–2277). Crucially, the two branches are **alternatives**, not an unconditional existence theorem. Later GP3 may compare complete candidates but is not the origin of the output.

This is also the precisely correct treatment of a *frozen but unrealizable* constructor: it does not prove `q` physically exists. Nor may an after-the-fact constructor be invented merely because it reproduces q.

### B4. No-idle closure

`p2:cor:no-idle-licensed-constructor` (3049–3081) is an immediate consequence of the no-omission, no-retyping and closure components of the GP2 realization-defect definition (2840–2873) and postulate (2877–3046). It is not a new operator-availability axiom; a mathematically definable but unlicensed operation remains outside `Gen_R(M)`.

## C. E3 / E4 verification

E3 independently models a finite frozen port signature as a typed set `R` and an exact representation as a type-preserving `Pres(M)`. Completeness is forced at every `r_i` by GP2; numerical equality does not identify incompatible sorts. If no exact realization is available, the source's `FRC` alternative is required.

E4 replay: the deterministic standard-library script `Paper2A-GeneratedConstituentsBatch37-v1_1-verify.py` passed **35/35 individual finite logical controls**. It checks missing ports, same-number/different-sort collisions, disallowed extra output, unlicensed or post-result constructors, wrong typed roots, absent joint roots, the nonobstruction branch and role-versus-token actualization. These tests do **not** establish existence of physical composite P, universal GP1 consistency, or physical occurrence of specific members.

## B39-2 — Backward proof slice, source clauses and exact quantifiers

1. **Freeze chronology and sorts:** `p2:def:intrinsic-generation-grammar` (1416–1443) and `p2a:def:frozen-constructor-origin-certificate` (2197–2230) distinguish a previously licensed typed constructor from an ambient mathematical operation. The defect definition requires `d_D∈Σ_R` **before candidate selection**, and invariance under unmarked role-isomorphisms. A candidate-dependent diagnostic can be generated inside `M`, but may not be promoted to earlier inventory `P_{<R}` as its own selection mark.
2. **Diagnostic role is not a GP2-failure condition:** `δ_D` can be the nonzero order-swap commutator, kernel, stabilizer, etc. Its target sort is part of the constructor. If it is generated and required, GP2 must preserve it. There is **no** general equation `δ_D=0` in GP2.
3. **Candidate realization:** `D_R^{GP2}(M)=(D_ins,D_omit,D_rel,D_type,D_clos)` is assessed against the **complete typed candidate-internal generated closure** `Gen_R(M)` and chronological prior inventory. The conditions are not five unrelated new principles. `D_clos` is intentionally diagnostic and may overlap `D_omit` and `D_type`.
4. **GP2 is stipulated at its actual constitutional scope:** `GP2_R(M) ⇔ D_R^{GP2}(M)=0` (2875–2890), expanded through four clauses (2894–2940). `Pres_M(M)=Gen_R(M)` at 2942–2949 is the **candidate-internal exactness subclause**; the no-insertion clause must still prevent promotion of candidate-private self-structure into prior comparison marks. The source explicitly excludes cross-candidate monotonicity at 2950–2960.
5. **No-idle corollary:** if a licensed `C` already has all generated inputs and declares required nonredundant output `y=C(inputs)`, then omission of `y` implies `D_clos ≠0`, usually also `D_omit` or `D_type` (3049–3081). It does not require outputs of unlicensed constructors, nor those whose declared prerequisites have not been generated.
6. **Failure branch:** a GP1-finite, GP2-exact realization may fail to exist. The source says to report an FRC obstruction, **not** modify the intrinsic defect to zero or append a new repair category (3083–3095). This is a role-specific conditional, not an independent physical gap.
7. **Rank-one example:** 8218–8243 consumes the already-established rank-one role/globalization proof. The nonzero commutator signature of the source-sensitive branch must be retained; the specific `SU(2)→SO(3)` quotient imposes a new central equality where the source-role presentation did not generate one. This relies on the **source's relation-exactness semantics**, not the invalid generic assertion that every quotient of any nonabelian group is forbidden.
8. **Direct import:** Paper II-F technical appendix (lines 107–116) cites both definitions and correctly distinguishes a generated diagnostic residual from a GP2 failure. Its later scalar-field stationarity calculation is a separate physical-action claim, not discharged by the defect definitions alone.

## B39-3 — Independent exact mathematical controls (E3)

**Nonzero intrinsic commutator.** On a finite complex two-dimensional carrier, choose

`X=((0,1),(1,0))`, `Z=((1,0),(0,-1))`. Then `XZ−ZX=((0,−2),(2,0))≠0`. If the commutator is a licensed generated diagnostic, a faithful realization must retain it; the **nonzero output is not an imperfection of the realization**.

**Quaternion quotient stress test.** In `Q8`, `[i,j]=−1≠1`. Under the central quotient `Q8/{±1}≅V4`, this element maps to the identity, and all quotient commutators vanish. An exact role requiring the original generated signed commutator is lost under that quotient. This is a finite **analogy** for the source's rank-one relation-fidelity argument; it is not a GP2-admissible countermodel to the SU(2) allocation.

**Closure is least generated content.** For a finite frozen grammar `root → τ → β`, the iterated closure of `{root}` is exactly `{root,τ,β}`. Removing `β` after `τ` has appeared violates a licensed required-output rule. Removing the `τ→β` schema from the frozen grammar, or withholding `τ`, removes this obligation. This proves the conditional no-idle inference by direct finite closure, not by physical intuition.

**Candidate exactness is not cross-candidate inclusion.** Two different complete presentations, e.g. native finite cyclic groups of orders 2 and 3, each may preserve its own generated multiplication and tests; `Gen(M₂)⊆Pres(M₁)` is not implied. This matches the source's explicit exclusion at 2950–2960. GP3's structured order is a separate comparison, not a consequence of GP2 exactness alone.

**Source-issued identity quotient.** If prior generated relations actually identify two labels, exactification may retain one equivalence class. If no such identification is licensed, deleting one label violates no-omission. This distinction depends on source-generated congruence, not the analyst's preference to compress.

## B39-4 — Adversarial claim-matched boundaries (E4)

- **Wrong-signature attack:** A quotient/compression that maps a generated nonzero commutator to zero fails `D_rel`; it is not licensed merely because the quotient is a mathematically familiar object. If the diagnostic is *not* licensed, the same raw algebraic commutator alone creates no physical GP2 requirement.
- **Wrong-sort attack:** retaining a required test as an isomorphic object-slot label fails `D_type`; a mathematical isomorphism does not erase the typed target requirement.
- **Missing-output attack:** absent output of a present, licensed, nonredundant constructor can trigger *both* `D_clos` and `D_omit`; the tuple does not require independent components.
- **Self-selection attack:** an invariant computed only inside a proposed candidate cannot be added to the pre-candidate inventory to select that candidate. This is the explicit no-insertion chronology rule.
- **FRC attack:** on a fixed one-dimensional complex algebra, every commutator vanishes. If a genuinely frozen role demands a nonzero commutator and **only** this carrier is allowed, no zero-realization-defect candidate exists; it is an obstruction, not a requirement to set the intrinsic defect to zero. No claim is made that the complete Born–Hopf candidate domain is restricted this way.
- **GP2 postulate status:** executable checks confirm elementary consequences of the declared semantics; they do **not** independently prove GP2, G0 occurrence, GP3 leastness, an entire physical carrier, or a new physical observable.

Reproducible controls: `Proofs/Paper2A-GP2DefectBatch39-v1_1-verify.py` and corresponding results. **47/47** finite exact controls passed. The count includes finite-model checks, not independent physics experiments or a formal soundness theorem for the complete framework.

## B. E1 backward proof replay, types and chronology

1. **GP2 is the actual parent premise.** In `p2:post:GP2`, lines 2986–3032, clause `(b)` asserts objectification only for a generated separating test spectrum with *its own faithful operation role and autonomous variance*; clause `(c)` asserts relation-free joint composition when there is **no prior generated cross-relation**. Line 3031–3032 specifies Cartesian product for the compact-Hausdorff parent presentation. The theorem at 3097–3158 is a scoped instantiation of these clauses, not their mathematical derivation from an unspecified group duality.
2. **Typed objectification.** The `B_A` operation law is contravariant/opposite relative to the active parent operation. Merely giving `B_A` a point-separating parametrization, without generated autonomous object/role typing, does not discharge clause (ii). The theorem does not equate `B_A` with an independently occurring G0 root or a second copy of `A` in the original carrier.
3. **Joint-support proof.** Clause (iii) is about a *frozen relation-slot item restricting simultaneously admissible pairs*. A total comparison map `A→B_A` or evaluation `A×B_A→Q` is a function/constructor between the types, not automatically a graph predicate narrowing the admissible support. GP2 no-insertion excludes an ungenerated graph condition; its explicit relation-free-composition clause selects `A×B_A`. The source result is a **role-support product**, not a statement that all possible physical preparations/events independently occur.
4. **Born exchange.** Only after a licensed isomorphism `β_A` is generated is the map `(a,b)→(β_A^{-1}(b),β_A(a))` defined on the product. Direct substitution gives its square as identity. The graph of `β_A` is its fixed-point subset, but is not thereby the full physical joint support. `p2a:cor:born-invertible-corealization` at 4482–4511 uses the same hypotheses and the canonical licensed operations; arbitrary co-realized nodes have no such involution without the bridge.
5. **Finite-image object typing.** `p2:lem:gp2-finite-generated-image-inheritance` (3160–3194) already appeared in the Batch 33 ledger. It produces only the finite nonredundant image `Z=im F` in the inherited **object** sort, not the entire ambient `Y` nor all induced derivations. Rechecking its premise avoids duplicate counts.

## C. E3 independent mathematical certificates

### C1. Nonabelian finite opposite role

Use `A=S_3` with multiplication `ab` and let `B_A=S_3^{op}`. The inversion map `β(a)=a^{-1}` is an isomorphism `S_3→S_3^{op}` because `β(ab)=b^{-1}a^{-1}=β(a)*_{op}β(b)`. On the 36-point full product, `B(a,b)=(b^{-1},a^{-1})` obeys `B²=id`. Exactly six pairs `(a,a^{-1})` are fixed and lie on the graph, whereas the full product has 36 pairs. Hence existence of `β` does not entail the graph support constraint. In the regular left action on the six-point group, the induced pullback on separating delta functions satisfies `P_{ab}=P_bP_a` and is faithful.

**This is a finite exact algebraic control, not a physical realization of the framework's particular reciprocal sphere.**

### C2. Finite generated object image in infinite ambient host

On the round `S³(R)⊂R⁴`, the gradients of the coordinate restrictions `x_i` are `V_i=e_i-(x_i/R²)x`. Their brackets are

`[V_i,V_j]=(x_i e_j−x_j e_i)/R²`, for `i<j`.

These six Killing vector fields are tangent, linearly independent and invariant under the host isometries. **The literal set image of the bilinear bracket `β` is not the full six-dimensional vector space**: in `\Lambda^2\mathbb R^4` it consists of decomposable bivectors and is a proper subset. The complete six-dimensional object `Z_β=span_R(im β)=im(\barβ)` follows only because addition/scalar multiplication are already constituted in the **parent section-object target**; alternatively Paper II-B gives a literal two-bracket constructor with exactly this set image (II-B 999–1056). The ambient vector-field space `Γ(TS³)` is infinite-dimensional (the fields `x_0^n[V_0,V_1]` are linearly independent for arbitrarily large `n`), and its full physical realization is **not** entailed by realizing the six-dimensional generated image. The induced derivations on smooth scalar functions are a distinct typed operation node when licensed.

The independent check tests the rank of the **generated linear hull**, not a false equality between `im β` and its span. It also tests host-type inheritance; it does not independently prove the **frozen physical constructor license** or give a second momentum G0 root.

### C3. Independent compactness theorem

The product of two compact Hausdorff spaces is compact Hausdorff. In the finite discrete `S_3` model all 36 pairs are allowed in the absence of a frozen constraint. A diagonal/graph constraint would reduce the support to six and would be a distinct presentation. This confirms the compact-Hausdorff product step, but the *physical* choice between those presentations comes from the declared GP2 relation slot, not topology alone.

- **Opposite variance absent:** A renamed point parametrization of `A` does not acquire a separate physical reciprocal sort. The autonomous-role premise fails; the theorem abstains.
- **Same-role internal image:** A canonical six-dimensional vector-field image with no generated autonomous role split remains a generated object within `R`. The opposite-variance reciprocal case is not captured by this no-splitting lemma.
- **Ambient operation availability:** Multiplication, tensoring or differentiation of arbitrary unlicensed ambient objects does not produce new physical carrier obligations; frozen licensed `F` and exact native-sort output are necessary.
- **Actuality vs role support:** `A×B_A` is the universal relation-free *role-level comparison support*, not a proof that arbitrary real-world physical event pairs or measured values occur.
- **Prior-versus-current chronology:** A candidate-private graph or symmetry cannot be promoted to the prior frozen inventory as its own selector. GP2's candidate-internal generated closure is assessed separately.
- **Universal Lie-parent portability:** A faithful finite dual of this specific generated reciprocal pair is not an all-candidate faithful affine action of every closed primitive parent. Hence the inherited `BH-IIA-PORT-001` issue is neither closed nor needed for these typed consequences.

The executable verifier `Proofs/Paper2A-ReciprocalRoleBatch40-v1_1-verify.py` uses exact finite `S_3` data and symbolic round-`S³` brackets. **1,705/1,705 individual exact assertions passed** in 16 labelled groups. These assertions are *not* independent experiments or a proof of GP2 itself.

## B41.3 — Independent proof chain: what actually establishes a common speed

The source deliberately uses two different operations:

1. **Support image:** if \(j_Q:\operatorname{Supp}(Q)\to \mathcal N_H\) is a nonzero host-equivariant map, its image is a nonzero invariant subobject of the frozen irreducible \(\mathcal N_H\). By the unique-irreducible-support hypothesis, \(\operatorname{im}j_Q=\mathcal N_H\). This establishes which host support the descendant reaches. It does **not**, by that argument alone, say that \(j_Q\) has zero kernel.
2. **Principal relation:** a distinct already licensed typed constructor must establish that the descendant's **order-two principal symbol** is inherited from the frozen normalized host owner and that no principal-order deformation has been generated. The previously audited `p2:thm:gp2-generated-owner-normalization-inheritance` then preserves its relative temporal/spatial coefficient. Up to an invertible zeroth-order endomorphism \(A_Q\), the conditional conclusion is

\[
\sigma_2(P_Q)(\tau,\xi)
=(\tau^2-c_{\rm op}^2 h^{-1}(\xi,\xi))A_Q.
\]

For a finite internal rank \(r\), \(\det \sigma_2(P_Q)=q_{\rm op}^{r}\det A_Q\). Since \(A_Q\) is invertible, the characteristic set coincides with \(q_{\rm op}=0\). **Only** on the separately assumed hyperbolic finite-propagation domain whose wavefront boundary is governed by that cone does the physical front-speed equality follow. A same-support operator \(\partial_t^2-c^2\Delta_h\) with \(c\ne c_{\rm op}\) violates principal inheritance, not the support theorem.

The later photon/scalar corollary is correctly conditional on a later **physical** photon or scalar channel and a certified undeformed owner-principal bridge. It does not establish measured light speed or choose units.

## B41.4 — Independent proofs for the remaining GP2 and compact-group claims

**Canonicity versus marked choice.** A licensed word whose outputs are all equivalent under an already frozen equivalence identifies a single unmarked orbit even if individual outputs have automorphisms. Conversely, choosing one of two inequivalent orbits would use data beyond the licensed word. Thus the source's `GP2` canonicity lemma is a **conditional no-new-choice criterion**, not a theorem licensing every mathematical universal construction.

**Repair theorem and its order guard.** If a replacement predicate is true of the same retained object it is a reclassification, not a new physical output. When a preorder has one maximal true element, that is a **greatest** element if every true element is bounded above by some maximal (automatically in a finite poset) or if the true set is directed. In the infinite poset \(\mathbb N\sqcup\{m\}\), with usual \(\mathbb N\) order and \(m\) incomparable to \(\mathbb N\), \(m\) is the sole maximal but not greatest: every integer has a successor and no integer is below \(m\). The theorem explicitly includes the necessary hypothesis and abstains otherwise. A new object from a licensed `Orig_Σ` word remains conditional on the original nonobstructed exact realization and typed required-output contract; uniqueness of the mathematical target is not a physical constructor.

**Constitutive fidelity.** Under the source's **explicit** exact equality \(\mathcal R(F_r)=\mathcal R_{\rm gen}\iff r=r_{\max}\), the exact GP2 premise eliminates all \(r<r_{\max}\). This is not numerical extremization by GP3. For an invertible finite matrix \(F\), every positive \(rF\) is injective, but only \(r=1\) equals a normalized relation fixed as \(F\). Therefore mere injectivity or positivity cannot produce the endpoint. The physical role may be a constitutive fidelity only if previously typed as one; a measured response amplitude is excluded by the theorem.

**Exact host closure.** At nonredundant generated typed presentation-item level, GP2 no-insertion gives \(\operatorname{Pres}^{\rm gen}\subseteq\operatorname{Adm}\), and no-omission plus host-faithfulness gives the reverse. This equality is a statement about generated **items/types**, not arbitrary multiplicity, concrete operator coefficients or physically occurring state members. If the exact generated closure cannot be GP1-realized, the actual exact candidate family is empty and FRC-obstructed. GP3 comparison occurs **after** this stage and does not minimize unmarked coefficients.

**Compact group connectedness.** For compact connected Hausdorff \(G\), the point-separating family of compact connected Lie quotients \(G_i\) and the maximal-torus surjectivity of the power map supply nonempty compact root preimages. Directed finite intersections and compactness provide an \(n\)-th root in \(G\). Conversely, the profinite quotient \(G/G_0\) inherits divisibility; every finite quotient of a divisible group is divisible, while no nontrivial finite group is divisible (choose \(n=|F|\)); hence \(G/G_0\) is trivial. This proves the printed equivalence without assuming **all full parents are Lie**. It does not itself license a physically occurring connected transfer root. The same-stage requirement is precisely what excludes a post-hoc root imported from another generated group.

## 4. E3 — independent mathematical replay

### 4.1 Rooted structural equivalence

Represent a finite chronological generated presentation by a typed directed multigraph of root nodes, constructor ports, output nodes, and declared incidences. Let `≡_Σ` be the congruence *generated by the frozen word's licensed identities*. In a GP2-exact realized presentation, every generated node has an image (no omission), every realized structural node has a generated preimage (no insertion, with all independent roots in the root tuple), and two source classes collapse only if `≡_Σ` already equates them (typed host/identity faithfulness). These are surjectivity, totality, and injectivity on the quotient. A finite structure-preserving bijection with preserved port incidence and operations is an isomorphism of the specified typed presentation sort. This proof is **conditional on the source's exact `RealStr` domain**; it does *not* claim a bijection between all mathematical derived theorems and physically occurring tokens.

Under an unresolved GP3 family, the family as a single generated role node and its common subpresentation may be carried through this isomorphism. A particular member remains unselected, consistent with the already integrated common-value/survivor repairs.

### 4.2 Incidence bridges and AND versus OR

For licensed constructor `w:(a,b)→x`, its typed incidence graph contains `a→w→x` even if b is absent as an independently considered root. **The arrow does not license x**; the physical constructor is available exactly when both inputs and its frozen `C(w)` have been certified. In a fixed finite DAG grammar, define `S_{n+1}=S_n∪{o(w):C(w),I(w)⊆S_n}`. By induction on the first insertion stage, every newly added node admits a finite rooted AND-tree. Conversely, induction on tree height places every internal output in some `S_n`. For alternatives `w1` or `w2` with the same output, a single complete AND-tree suffices, but the existence of one route does not select a GP3 member.

A root `r` is indispensable for output x **within that frozen word grammar** precisely when x is derivable from R but not from `R\{r}`. A drawn path from r to x is neither sufficient for generated output nor proof of root indispensability. The theorem expressly freezes the constructor and C predicates across the deletion test; if one changes the actual physical grammar, it is a different comparison.

### 4.3 Neutral ancestry and retained propagation

For an equivariant `j:V→N` with irreducible nonzero image `N`, `im j=N` does **not** imply `ker j=0` (e.g. `j:C²→C`, `(a,b)↦a`). The patched corollary's `Supp_H(Q)` names the host-visible image and separately guards full-source injectivity. No new kinetic normalization is inferred: inherited front speed still consumes a licensed same-owner undeformed principal operator relation and hyperbolic wavefront-domain premise.

### 4.4 Lie-port repair exact domain

From any compact connected noncommutative H, a commutator-separating Peter–Weyl representation has a compact connected **Lie image K**, with nonzero semisimple Lie algebra. The witness image may not be relation-exact. The **existing GP3 definition** admits its retained germ only after `E_adm`, whose nonempty compact exact class can be witnessed by `T^r×S`, and whose members retain the simply connected semisimple source subgroup S. Each contains an embedded simple-root `SU(2)` and admits a complete endpoint. This witnesses the candidatewise GP3 route independently of a faithful finite representation of H itself. For a rich `Geo_R` with extra constituted directions or tests, the endpoint comparison remains conditional on preserving those diagrams. This proof does not produce a universal Lie-port theorem or an empirical transfer token.

## 4. E3 independent mathematical replay and exact counter-shortcuts

1. **Born product and fixed graph.** For any bijection `β:A→B`, the exchange `B_β(a,b)=(β^{-1}(b),β(a))` squares to identity on the *full* Cartesian product. Its fixed-point set is exactly the graph `{(a,β(a))}`—a smaller set for `|A|>1`. GP2's relation-free product cannot be replaced by that graph unless the independent generated support relation exists. The generated isomorphism alone does not select a physical preparation.
2. **Equivariance vs invariance.** On `R²`, let the Born involution swap coordinates and define a typed two-component readout `w(x,y)=(x+y,x-y)`. It obeys `w(y,x)=diag(1,-1)w(x,y)` yet is not invariant on non-fixed inputs. This makes the theorem's target-action qualifier logically necessary. If `x=y`, the odd output is zero. A set `{+1,-1}` can be stable under sign exchange while neither of its two members is fixed, preventing the invalid passage from invariant-family object to selected fixed member.
3. **Marked transport.** On successive typed vector spaces `V_j`, invertible maps `B_j` and licensed constructors `C_j` with `C_j^B B_{j-1}=B_j C_j` yield `C_m^B…C_1^B B_0=B_m C_m…C_1`. This does not identify the original presentation with its marked reciprocal one. Missing a transported word or a commuting square prevents the inference; physical forward transport is not manufactured by a mathematical isomorphism.
4. **Dual-operation hierarchy needs stability.** With `X={0,1}`, effects `E={(0,0),(0,1)}⊂{0,1}^X`, evaluation separates effects and the effect `(0,1)` distinguishes states. The map `T` swapping the two points sends that effect to `(1,0)∉E`: no dual `E→E` satisfies the evaluation equation. Thus bare separating evaluation does **not** imply an evaluation-dual operation; the already printed stabilizer theorem `(ED-STAB)` requires `P_T(j(E))⊆j(E)`. Even when stable mathematically, a predeclared physical operation certificate is distinct, and reciprocal objectification needs its GP2 clauses. This disproves only the unguarded *reading of a schematic remark* and motivates its clarification; the actual guarded theorems are not refuted.
5. **Identity vs equivalence.** Two abstract groups or modules may be isomorphic but have different generated owner/provenance. A generated same-sort identity congruence collapses labels; an isomorphism or numerical equality does not. The structural manifestation/deduplication corollaries are scoped precisely to the generated identity sort, not to arbitrary functorial mathematical equivalences.
6. **Empty next-stage countercontrol.** Let an already nonempty finite exact candidate set be `M_n={0,1}`. An independently frozen later frontier adds two exact tests with admissible loci `A={0}`, `B={1}`. Then `M_{n+1}=M_n∩A∩B=∅`. This is a legitimate mathematical test of a licensed candidate-filter problem's **FRC-obstructed possibility**, not an assertion that those contradictory tests both physically occur in a completed Born–Hopf model. The former unqualified arrow `P_n→P_{n+1}` has no physical terminal object to denote. The source's own FRC alternative applies and leaves `P_n` intact. If the intersection is nonempty, relative GP comparison proceeds on that *complete* family; if it has multiple irreducible survivors, no arbitrary member is chosen.
7. **Empty frontier fixed point.** On a nonobstructed already closed stage, adding no generated requirements changes neither its GP1/GP2 exact realization class nor its GP3 comparisons; hence no new stage information is produced, as the fixed-point corollary states. This result is independent of and compatible with the new obstructed-extension branch.

## B44.3 — Independent E3: the decisive mathematical statements

### Spin(4) word length is exactly total weight

Represent irreducibles by `V_(a/2,b/2)` with nonnegative integers `a,b`. Every fundamental tensor factor changes **one** SU(2) highest-weight index by at most one; after `r` factors, `a+b≤r`. Conversely, a word containing exactly `a` left fundamentals and `b` right fundamentals contains the highest-spin constituent `V_(a/2,b/2)` by repeated SU(2) Clebsch–Gordan decomposition. Thus its minimum length is exactly `r=a+b`, i.e. `ℓ(V_(jL,jR))=2jL+2jR`. The union of all word factors up to `2m` has precisely the support `jL+jR≤m` as the statement at 6078–6101 claims. This calculation is independent of the paper's argument and makes no statement about whether the physical grammar actually contains every word.

At the **mathematical** budget `m=1`, the possible types are `(0,0)`, `(1/2,0)`, `(0,1/2)`, `(1/2,1/2)`, `(1,0)`, `(0,1)` with dimensions `1+2+2+4+3+3=15`. This matches, but does not *generate*, the established initial `H1`. The identity must not be inverted to make the optional tensor grammar the constitutional origin of `m_p=1`.

### The bracket image is not its linear hull

On `S³(R)` let `f_u(x)=u·x`; then `∇f_u=u−(u·x)x/R²` and, on the sphere, `[∇f_u,∇f_v]=(f_u v−f_v u)/R²`. These fields are infinitesimal ambient rotations. The linearized map `Λ²R⁴→so(4)` is an isomorphism and therefore the **real linear hull** of the bracket outputs has dimension six. But a single decomposable `u∧v` has skew rank at most two, while the perfectly valid rotation `e₁∧e₂+e₃∧e₄` has skew rank four and is not one bracket. II-B expressly distinguishes these claims, and the section-object target has already constituted addition. The actual physical import is precisely that licensed target-vector-span output, not an arbitrary higher tensor constructor.

### Static finite algebra cannot recover a parent generator

For finite Hermitian `H` and an injective relabeling `f` of its spectrum, Lagrange interpolation provides `g` with `g(f(H))=H`, so `C*(H)=C*(f(H))` although the numerical eigenvalues can differ. If `f` is not injective, distinct spectral projections can merge. Thus an algebra-only input cannot identify which parent differential-order/dispersion/symbol is meant. A generated parent filtration and compression map are extra **declared data**, not deducible from the algebra.

### Finite versus asymptotic targets

An error bound `r_B(m)→0` does not assert eventual exact vanishing; for instance `r_B(m)=1/(m+1)` is positive at every finite level. Conversely a genuine fixed-band exact identity implies zero error on that band beyond a stated threshold. The admitted status must be read from the actual theorem and remainder, not from the adjective “large”. A sequence of **finite** algebraic frontiers can have unbounded least harmonic resolution across its length without realizing an infinite-dimensional frontier at any individual stage.

## 45.3 — E3 independent mathematical proofs and boundary attacks

### Connectedness without an ambient Lie port

For connected `C⊂K` containing identity `e`, its finite product `C^n` is connected because multiplication is continuous and `C^n` is the continuous image of the connected product space `C×…×C`. Each `C^n` contains `e` and is contained in `C^(n+1)`, so the union is connected, and so is its closure. If this closure is the compact topological parent, it is connected. The argument requires no finite matrix representation. Conversely, connectedness would *not* follow from a separate discrete seed; absence of that seed is part of the stated primitive input. A nontrivial effective action supplies `K≠{e}`. The Lie label is not inferred.

### Total boundedness is stronger than finite diameter and weaker than compact X

For any nonempty metric space `X`, total boundedness gives a finite `1`-net and hence finite diameter by the triangle inequality. The converse fails: the countably infinite discrete metric `d(i,j)=1` for distinct integers has diameter 1 but arbitrarily large `1/2`-separated subsets, so it is not totally bounded. Conversely `(0,1)∩Q` is totally bounded but noncomplete and noncompact; its canonical completion is `[0,1]`. A compact interval has distances accumulating at zero and no positive minimum separation. Therefore source claims `N_X(eps)<∞`, compact `Xhat`, and *not* compact/finite `X` follow exactly as printed.

### Rate family and synchronized composition

If all `v(Phi)` are finite and `V_prop` is totally bounded in the ordinary real metric, then `sup V_prop=c_op<∞`; each pairwise ratio is bounded by `v(Phi)` and hence by `c_op`. With jointly continuous metric `d`, limits of common-bounded trajectories inherit the inequality; for finitely many matched elapsed junctions, triangle inequalities add intervals so the *same* `c_op` remains sufficient. A locally finite collection of junctions intersects any compact elapsed interval finitely. If a flow satisfies `Phi_(t+s)=Phi_t Phi_s` over all real t, then `(Phi_t)^n=Phi_(nt)`. Substitution `u=nt,w=ns` gives exactly `v((Phi_t)^n)=n v(Phi)`; hence a nonstationary full synchronized family cannot have bounded rate image. This says nothing about an **unlicensed** operation or about a constructor accounting `n` elapsed durations rather than one: those are different timed signatures.

On `S¹` with geodesic arc metric and ordinary time, `Phi_t(z)=e^{iωt}z` has `v=|ω|`; its spatial group is compact for any ω, while the rate family for ω=n is unbounded. Thus compactness of the group does not prove the finite timed ceiling. For `V_prop={1-1/n:n≥1}`, the closure is `{1-1/n:n≥1} ∪ {1}`; the supremum 1 exists as the closure maximum yet is not attained by any member. This exactly validates the source's closure-versus-attainment qualifier.

### Skeleton projection is **not** an additional selection proof

The source's `S_1 \preceq_R^{sk} S_2` notation is defined by the **existence** of compared complete lifts. In general mathematical preorders, existential projection onto equivalence classes with more than one lift does not itself guarantee transitivity unless comparison respects the projection (the source does *not* use it to select a particular lift). For example, abstract full candidates `a,b₁,b₂,c` with the only nontrivial comparisons `a≤b₁` and `b₂≤c`, and with `b₁,b₂` projecting to the same skeleton, yield projected `A≤B` and `B≤C` but not `A≤C`. **This abstract toy does not establish such a failure inside `Adm_R`**, whose primitive comparison is explicitly built from skeleton chains. The full candidatewise GP3 preorder and the separately proved candidatewise endpoint reachability remain the controlling statements. **Audit guard:** do not use mere existence of different intermediary lifts to infer transitivity/unique carrier selection; when actually needed, check that the chain admits a complete compatible lift at the intermediate skeleton. No new certified source gap, physical countermodel or theorem failure follows from this general control.

### Chronological endpoint examples

An unmarked scalar role might admit a complete U1 reduction of an internally exact T² candidate, but a role whose independently prior Geo_R requires two toral directions does not. Likewise a primitive order-sensitive role may admit a complete root-SU2 endpoint below a valid SU3 candidate, but an SU3-specific constituted bracket/test forbids that comparison. The SO3 comparison differs: source `E_adm(su2)` uses GP2 prior-relation exactness to prohibit an added central quotient when the compact SU2 source integration is available. It does **not** ban every SO3 instance in an unrelated signature. These cases validate the source's explicit chronological quantifiers instead of introducing a dimension minimization.
